# Game facts

STATUS: CONTRACT (one row per fact, held by GameFactsContractTests in every repo on the rulebook)

What FFT: The Ivalice Chronicles really does, in one list every mod reads. Each row is one thing
the game does, how sure we are of it, what showed it, and which mods break if it is wrong. A
mod's own tricks and habits are not game facts, so they stay in that mod's repo.

The newest list is this file on main in FFTModGates. A mod's `gates/docs/GAME_FACTS.md` is the
list as of the rulebook version it took.

## Who writes it

One session writes this file, the fact keeper in FFTModGates. Every other session sends it facts
and never edits the file. A message carries the fact in plain words, the mark it claims, the
evidence, and the repos that lean on it. The keeper merges it under the rules below and commits.

## A row

| Column | What it holds |
|---|---|
| Id | A short kebab case name in brackets, never reused. Cite a fact by it, as `[unit-move-jump-bytes]`. |
| Fact | The claim, plain words first, then the offsets, ids and addresses. One claim per row. |
| Mark | One of the five marks below. |
| Evidence | What showed it. Each source opens with its kind and a colon and ends with the repo and day it came from in brackets, as `(BB, 2026-10-26)`. Sources are split by semicolons. |
| Leaned on by | The repos whose code or plans rest on it, short names from the repo table, comma separated. |

A cell never holds a pipe character, and a source never holds a semicolon of its own.

## The five marks

- PROVEN: a session saw it hold in the running game, and Patrick agreed. The evidence carries a
  live source and either "Patrick agreed YYYY-MM-DD" or, for a mark brought in from a repo whose
  proven mark was already his call, "proven in BB YYYY-MM-DD" or "proven in LW YYYY-MM-DD".
- UNCERTAIN: read off a tape, a table, the code or someone else's work, or seen live without
  Patrick's agreement yet. Fine to build on with care, but not settled.
- CONTRADICTED: the claim as written does not hold on this build, or the evidence points both
  ways. The evidence says what overturned it. Probe before building on it.
- WALLED: looked into and blocked. Do not retry without new information.
- NOT MODELED: a factor the game has that a tool meant to account for it, today the Battle Brain
  simulator, does not model yet. The row records the gap, not a truth.

## Evidence kinds

| Kind | What it is | Cite as |
|---|---|---|
| live tape | A recorded live session, read during or after it | the tape file and `#<seq>` |
| live watch | Seen in the running game, with or without a log | the log file, or "no log" |
| game table | A table read from the game's data files or from memory | the table and where it lives |
| Ghidra read | The exe's code read on disk, in Ghidra or any disassembler | the function or RVA |
| exe scan | A byte pattern or a file image probe run on the exe without launching it | the pattern or the probe |
| harness run | An automated battle run | the run, the battle and `#<seq>` |
| source read | Someone else's code or notes, not run here | the project and file |
| PSX doc | The FFHacktics wiki or another write up of the PSX game, not checked on this build | the page |

## Builds

An address or offset holds for the build its evidence names. With no build named it is the 1.5.2
no-DRM build (exe sha256 937233f7fe76182a..., image stamp 0x6A5E4A1B). Addresses are preferred
addresses (image base 0x140000000) or RVAs, as their source wrote them.

The 1.5.2 build with its copy protection, the Denuvo build, counts as this build for the address
a row names, since every RVA Living Weapons pinned there carried over unchanged
([denuvo-free-build-relocates-under-aslr]). Such a row says "on the Denuvo build" in its evidence.

## How a fact gets merged

1. One claim per row. A claim with a game half and a tool half is split, and the tool half stays
   in its repo.
2. The same fact from two places is one row carrying both sources.
3. A later look that overturns a row marks it CONTRADICTED and says what overturned it. The row
   stays, and its id is never reused.
4. The keeper flips a row to PROVEN once a session saw it hold live and Patrick agreed, and writes
   the day he agreed. Nobody hands the flip back to him.
5. A mark brought in from a repo keeps its word only when it meets this list's rules. A layout
   fact proven only on an older build comes in UNCERTAIN with its old proof named. A script's
   SELF PROVEN comes in UNCERTAIN, cited as a harness run.

| Short | Repo |
|---|---|
| BB | FFTBattleBrain, the battle AI mod |
| sim | The Battle Brain simulator, tools/sim in FFTBattleBrain |

## Facts

### The AI decision call

| Id | Fact | Mark | Evidence | Leaned on by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [battle-routine-entry] | The game asks what a computer run unit does next through one routine, BattleRoutine(unit index, action request), returning a 64 bit code, at RVA 0x31F130. | PROVEN | exe scan: MSVC form pattern, one hit at RVA 0x31F130 (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: hooked there, probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: no log, fftivc.together hooked there in step 0 (BB, 2026-10-26); source read: dicene/fftivc.together Mod.cs (BB, 2026-10-26); source read: skeewirt/fftivc-symbol-map, the WotL symbol _Z13BattleRoutineiP13ActionRequest (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [battle-routine-unit-index-is-slot] | The routine's first argument is the acting unit's slot in the battle unit array. | PROVEN | live watch: acting.ptr equals actor_ptr on every call, seq 7 to 12, probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [battle-routine-return-two-reasks] | Returning 2 from the routine means not decided yet. The game asks again once a frame, about every 16.6 ms, keeps running, and leaves the request buffer as it was. Returning 0 means decided, and the request in the buffer runs as written. | PROVEN | live watch: 189 asks over 3.2 s with the buffer identical on all 189, seq 56 to 248, probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: no log, step 0, edited and unedited requests ran after fftivc.together's hold (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: a move to (6,7) written into a held request walked there, brain/probe_20261026_230258.jsonl, a log lost on 2026-10-27 (BB, 2026-10-26); source read: dicene/fftivc.together Mod.cs (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [battle-routine-covers-every-ai-turn] | The same routine decides enemy turns, a player unit on the game's Auto-Battle, and a player unit whose human bit was cleared from outside. | PROVEN | live watch: seq 14 to 24 of probe_20261026_212759.jsonl and session probe_20261026_214658 (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: no log, the owner's Archer and Knight, cleared at entry, went through it from their first turn in the third battle of 2026-10-26 evening (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [battle-routine-prologue-no-rip-operand] | The routine's opening 26 bytes do not depend on where they sit in memory: the first instruction that does, a cmp [rip+x], sil, starts at byte 26. The opening is 48 89 5C 24 08 48 89 74 24 10 48 89 7C 24 18 41 57 48 83 EC 20 33 F6 48 63 F9. | UNCERTAIN | exe scan: the body at RVA 0x31F130 on 1.5.2 no-DRM (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB |
| [battle-routine-direct-call-site] | The routine has one direct caller in .text, a call (E8) at RVA 0x20ADC6, and no jump thunk in .text leads to it. Indirect calls and other sections were not scanned, and the caller has not been seen live. | UNCERTAIN | exe scan: rel32 E8 and E9 scan of .text (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB |
| [battle-routine-runs-on-game-thread] | The game calls the routine on a thread of its own, neither the mod loader's thread nor the first thread started. Which loop drives that thread is not known. | PROVEN | live watch: thread 60412 against loader thread 60112 and first started thread 58132, probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [battle-routine-one-call-per-phase] | The game calls the routine once for each phase of a turn and does not ask again when the answer comes at once. An enemy turn with no target in range is one Move request, then one EndTurn request about 1.5 to 2.3 s later, after the walk. | PROVEN | live watch: probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [stock-routine-decides-under-a-millisecond] | The game's own routine decides in well under a millisecond, about 0.26 ms for a Move and 0.002 ms for an EndTurn. | PROVEN | live watch: probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [battle-routine-hold-no-time-limit] | The game sets no time limit on a not decided answer. It kept asking once a frame through holds of 70 s (4,205 asks) and 78.6 s (4,725 asks), ran the released decision normally, and its current action globals held still throughout. | PROVEN | live watch: a 70 s hold on slot 4, brain/probe_20261026_230258.jsonl, a log lost on 2026-10-27 (BB, 2026-10-26); live tape: first_live_battle_hold_fold_and_handbrake#4735 (BB, 2026-10-27); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [com-command-mode-routine] | A routine at RVA 0x202F34 runs when the game puts a unit into computer command mode, Battle_Set_ComCommandMode in fftivc.together. Its return type is unknown, and the double together declares is suspect. | UNCERTAIN | source read: dicene/fftivc.together (BB, 2026-10-26); exe scan: MSVC form pattern, one hit at RVA 0x202F34 (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB |
| [current-action-byte] | A byte at preferred 0x141872EC4 reads 0 before a unit's Move decision and 255 after it, and stays 255 through the EndTurn request. It sits at spd_back + 0x1824, spd_back being RVA 0x18716A0, and fftivc.together calls it CurrentAction. | PROVEN | live watch: probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); exe scan: the spd_back lea, one hit at RVA 0x316844 resolving to RVA 0x18716A0 (BB, 2026-10-26); source read: dicene/fftivc.together (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [current-action-zero-forces-new-decision] | Writing 0 to the current action byte before calling the routine again makes the game's own AI decide the turn afresh. | UNCERTAIN | source read: dicene/fftivc.together, which does this before re-running the stock routine (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB |

The record then reads the real tile again. ### The action request

| Id | Fact | Mark | Evidence | Leaned on by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [action-request-move-destination] | A Move request (byte +0 is 0) carries the destination tile's X at +4 and Y at +8, each a u16. | PROVEN | live watch: three walks ended on the requested tile by the unit's grid bytes +0x4F and +0x50, probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: no log, step 0, Y edited to 0x0E walked to the edited tile (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: a written move to (6,7) walked there, brain/probe_20261026_230258.jsonl, a log lost on 2026-10-27 (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB, sim |
| [action-request-facing-byte] | Byte +C of a Move request holds the unit's facing, 0 to 3, and the same value rides into the EndTurn request of a turn that moved first, where writing it sets the facing the unit ends its turn with. | PROVEN | live watch: the step 0 edit and the seq 29 overwrite, probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB, sim |
| [action-request-type-byte] | The action request is 24 bytes, and byte +0 names what it asks for: 0 Move, 1 Act, 2 EndTurn. | PROVEN | live watch: every request in probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); source read: dicene/fftivc.together ActionRequest.cs for the 24 byte layout (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB, sim |
| [act-request-target-tile] | An Act request carries its target tile's X at +0x10 and Y at +0x14, each a u16. Bytes +0x08 to +0x0B read 0 on an Act, so together's Field_8 label for the target does not hold. | PROVEN | live watch: four Acts checked against the victims' grid bytes, probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: no log, human enemies' Acts in the second battle of 2026-10-26 evening (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB, sim |
| [act-request-skillset-and-ability] | An Act request names its skillset in byte +0x05 and its ability in the u16 at +0x06. A basic Attack is skillset 0x01 ability 0, and a Knight's Arts of War showed skillset 0x07 with abilities 0x8A, 0x8B and 0x91. | PROVEN | live watch: probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: no log, the second battle of 2026-10-26 evening (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB, sim |
| [act-request-plus-0c-word] | The u16 at +0x0C of an Act request is non zero when a humanoid unit attacks (0x1E, 0x25, 0x5A and 0x5B seen) and 0 when a monster does. What it means is open, a score or the damage being the guesses. | PROVEN | live watch: probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: no log, a Knight's and an Archer's attacks in the second battle of 2026-10-26 evening (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [act-request-target-slot-byte] | Byte +0x0F of an Act request often holds the target's slot in the unit array when a humanoid unit attacks, but reads 0xFF on monsters' attacks and on some humanoid ones, so the tile at +0x10 and +0x14 is the reliable target. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: seq 27, 41 and 46 of probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: no log, the second battle of 2026-10-26 evening (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB |
| [act-request-unmapped-bytes] | On every Act request seen, byte +0x0E reads 5 and the u16 at +0x16 reads 0xFFFF, while the low byte of +0x04 varies (0x00, 0x03, 0x04, 0x10 and 0x11 seen). What these bytes mean is open. | PROVEN | live watch: probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [move-request-plus6-not-height] | The u16 at +6 of a Move request is not the destination's height, whatever together's label Destination_Elevation says: the game's own moves wrote 0 there on all 223 seen, on four maps whose destinations stand at heights 0 to 8. | PROVEN | live tape: first_driver_battle_stub_then_opus_plays_the_enemy#7 to #49731 (BB, 2026-10-27); live tape: exam_two_battle_one_unwatched_auto_battle#7 to #102 (BB, 2026-10-27); live tape: exam_two_battle_two_orbonne_fourth_level_unwatched#7 to #106 (BB, 2026-10-27); live tape: first_live_battle_hold_fold_and_handbrake, 87 moves (BB, 2026-10-27); Patrick agreed 2026-10-28 | BB, sim |
| [move-request-plus6-may-be-level] | The u16 at +6 of a Move request may be the level of the destination tile, 0 for the lower and 1 for the upper. Every stock move seen went to a level 0 tile, so it is not yet told apart from always 0. A stock move onto a raised deck (MAP006, MAP043 or MAP064) would settle it. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: first_driver_battle_stub_then_opus_plays_the_enemy#7 to #49731 and three more tapes, 223 stock moves all to level 0 tiles (BB, 2026-10-27) | BB, sim |
| [endturn-facing-byte-after-act-first] | After a turn that acted before it moved, byte +C of the EndTurn request is not a facing: it read 36 on both such turns, against 0 to 3 on every turn that moved first. | PROVEN | live tape: first_live_battle_hold_fold_and_handbrake#4775 (BB, 2026-10-27); live tape: first_live_battle_hold_fold_and_handbrake#4883 (BB, 2026-10-27); Patrick agreed 2026-10-28 | BB, sim |
| [request-buffer-reused-across-turns] | The game keeps one request buffer from turn to turn: a turn's first request still holds the previous turn's type (2) and facing, with only the coordinates zeroed. | PROVEN | live watch: probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [move-request-answered-with-endturn] | Answering a Move request with an EndTurn ends the turn there with the unit unmoved, and the unit's next turn opens as normal. | PROVEN | live tape: s1_conversions_hand_mode_araguay_woods_ending_in_petrify#121 (BB, 2026-10-28); live tape: s1_conversions_hand_mode_araguay_woods_ending_in_petrify#138 (BB, 2026-10-28); live tape: s1_conversions_hand_mode_araguay_woods_ending_in_petrify#308, at the Move after an Act (BB, 2026-10-28); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [act-request-answered-with-move] | Answering an Act request with a Move walks the unit, and the game then asks for the Act again, so the act is put off, not dropped. | PROVEN | live tape: s1_conversions_hand_mode_araguay_woods_ending_in_petrify#271 (BB, 2026-10-28); live tape: s1_conversions_hand_mode_araguay_woods_ending_in_petrify#273 (BB, 2026-10-28); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [move-request-answered-with-act] | Answering a Move request with an Act runs the Act from where the unit stands, and the game then asks for the Move the unit still has. | PROVEN | live watch: no log, slot 2's attack in the second battle of 2026-10-26 evening (BB, 2026-10-26); live tape: s1_conversions_hand_mode_araguay_woods_ending_in_petrify#305 and #308 (BB, 2026-10-28); live tape: s1_retry_act_first_follow_up_move_written_as_act#158 and #160 (BB, 2026-10-28); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [act-over-endturn-after-stay] | An Act written over the EndTurn request that follows a stay, a Move to the unit's own tile, runs, and the game then asks for a Move again and the unit walks. A stay does not spend the move. | PROVEN | live tape: s1_conversions_hand_mode_araguay_woods_ending_in_petrify#403, #406 and #407 (BB, 2026-10-28); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [second-act-over-follow-up-move] | After a unit has acted, an Act written over its follow-up Move request still plays: the same attack started again and ended with no effect, the unit did not move, and the game asked for EndTurn next. | PROVEN | live tape: s1_retry_act_first_follow_up_move_written_as_act#160 (BB, 2026-10-28); live tape: s1_retry_act_first_follow_up_move_written_as_act#161 (BB, 2026-10-28); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [act-request-answered-with-endturn] | Answering an Act request the game's AI asked for itself with an EndTurn ends the turn there: the unit does nothing and the battle goes on with no freeze. | PROVEN | live tape: hand_recipe_ends_the_turn_in_place_of_the_leftover_act#61 and #64 (BB, 2026-10-29); live tape: leftover_act_out_of_reach_declined_as_end_turn#91 and #93 (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [act-over-endturn-after-walk] | An Act written over the EndTurn request that follows a real walk runs: a bird walked one tile, the game asked to end the turn, and a Talon Dive written over that request landed on Ramza. | PROVEN | live tape: hand_recipes_walk_then_act_and_leftover_act_not_learned#84 and #87 (BB, 2026-10-29); live watch: no log, the owner saw it land (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [illegal-request-is-no-op] | The game treats any illegal action request as a no op or a re-ask, never a crash. That holds for Move only: the game checks a Move's destination, an off map tile a no op and an unreachable one asked again, but trusts an Act completely, and a basic attack written from four tiles away ran. | CONTRADICTED | live watch: no log, the BB-10 session behind the standing correction, a basic attack from four tiles away executed (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB |
| [act-request-target-level-word] | The u16 at +0x12 of an Act request is the target's level, 0 for the lower and 1 for the upper. All 12 recorded acts with a 1 on a level 1 unit played. | PROVEN | live tape: venom_fang_lands_after_the_level_fix_then_a_new_map_clears_the_lists_live#1727 with the owner's screen, the attack seen landing after the fix (BB, 2026-11-03); live tape: a_ground_target_left_at_level_one_soft_locks_a_rider_live and entice_a_counter_kill_and_the_last_enemy_to_a_crystal_game_over_live (BB, 2026-11-03); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |
| [ground-target-level-one-soft-locks] | An Act aimed at a unit on the ground with its target level word at +0x12 set to 1 soft locks the game: both such plans on the recordings locked. A 0 aimed at a level 1 unit played. Charge time is not the cause: Hesitation, charge time 5, written over a Move request at a bridge unit played and the unit then walked from (3,2) to (2,2). | PROVEN | live tape: a_ground_target_left_at_level_one_soft_locks_a_rider_live and entice_a_counter_kill_and_the_last_enemy_to_a_crystal_game_over_live, the locks at seq 15845 and seq 2981 of those tapes and Hesitation at seq 13081, with the owner's screen, replacing a same day reading that blamed the charge time (BB, 2026-11-03); live tape: venom_fang_lands_after_the_level_fix_then_a_new_map_clears_the_lists_live#1727, game 1 and plan 0 at a ground unit played and the owner saw it land (BB, 2026-11-03); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |
| [act-ownership-not-checked] | The game does not check that a unit owns the ability an Act request names: a job 104 Coeurl threw polearms, skillset 20 ability 389, because a plan said so. | PROVEN | live tape: entice_a_counter_kill_and_the_last_enemy_to_a_crystal_game_over_live#12687 with the owner's screen (BB, 2026-11-03); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |
| [act-request-skillset-two-no-act] | An Act request whose skillset byte reads 2 is no act of its own but a leftover: all 9 on the recordings came from the game itself, 8 holding the ability id of the previous unit's act and the ninth the Claw played on an earlier turn. A Claw taught from one to a Mystic played with no visible effect. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: venom_fang_lands_after_the_level_fix_then_a_new_map_clears_the_lists_live, a_ground_target_left_at_level_one_soft_locks_a_rider_live and entice_a_counter_kill_and_the_last_enemy_to_a_crystal_game_over_live, the ninth at seq 1904 and the Mystic's Claw at seq 2972 (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [opening-endturn-answered-as-move-or-act] | An EndTurn that is a unit's first request of its turn can be answered with anything. Answered as an Act, it plays: a goblin's Tackle took Ramza 600 to 420. Answered as a Move, to the unit's own tile or another (a goblin walked (7,4) to (8,4)), the unit moves and the game asks End again, and an Act written over that End plays too. Each such turn closed with the game asking End. | PROVEN | live tape: brain\probe_20261105_120504.jsonl#474 to #507, #797 to #811 and #1806 to #1841, clips probe_20261105_120504-seq505.mp4, -seq807.mp4 and -seq1839.mp4 in F:\FFTRec\clips, the session log no longer kept (BB, 2026-11-05); live tape: brain\probe_20261105_130316.jsonl#14331 to #14966, a move then act and an act only on End opened turns, the session log no longer kept (BB, 2026-11-05); Patrick agreed 2026-11-05 | BB |
| [written-act-ignores-height-window] | An Act written into the request plays from outside the skill's height window: of 65 written range 1, vertical 2 orders outside it, at -9 to +12 half steps, 63 set the acted byte and 1 was bounced, the game asking again. Of the 50 damaging ones 36 landed against 36.5 expected, and 34 matched the forecast exactly. The code that plays a written request was not read. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: 823 written orders in the 46 kept session files, written_reach_scan.py and window_binds.py in knock_lone_2026-11-09 (BB, 2026-11-09) | BB |
| [endturn-facing-write-rarely-holds] | A facing written on a monster's EndTurn request, on a turn with no act, rarely holds: of 105 changed writes pointing off the nearest foe rule the unit ended the written way once, the rule's way 92 times and neither 12. The writes that seemed to take, 128 of 172 for west, north or east, mostly matched the rule anyway, and south took 0 of 67. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: the 27 session files of sim_cast_2026-11-08, docs/research/sim_playout_2026-11-09.txt (BB, 2026-11-09) | BB |

### The battle unit record

| Id | Fact | Mark | Evidence | Leaned on by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [unit-move-jump-bytes] | A unit's Move and Jump, gear and movement abilities counted, are bytes +0x42 and +0x43 of its battle unit record. | PROVEN | live tape: gear_battle_four_known_move_and_jump_pairs_at_dial_100.jsonl.gz#10 (BB, 2026-11-01); PSX doc: FFHacktics wiki Battle Stats (BB, 2026-11-01); Patrick agreed 2026-11-01 | BB, sim |
| [unit-human-control-bit] | Bit 0x08 of a unit's Flags2, at +0x05 and mirrored at +0x1EE, means a human picks its actions. Setting it on both bytes from outside opens the player's menu on an enemy, and clearing it hands the turn to the AI. | PROVEN | live tape: live_check_act_first_walks_and_mid_turn_loss_retries_clean#2585 (BB, 2026-10-29); live watch: Unit Control cleared it on both bytes of both player units at battle entry, 0x0B to 0x03 and 0x08 to 0x00, and the AI played them, session probe_20261026_214658 (BB, 2026-10-26); source read: dicene/fftivc.unitcontrol BattleUnits.cs (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: FFTMultiplayer PuppetHold held a whole enemy team human for a battle on 1.5 (BB, 2026-10-26); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB, sim, MP |
| [battle-unit-array-location] | The battle units are 21 records of 0x200 bytes in one array at preferred 0x141853CE0, BattleUnits__bwork in fftivc.unitcontrol. | UNCERTAIN | source read: dicene/fftivc.unitcontrol BattleUnits.cs (BB, 2026-10-26); exe scan: unitcontrol's pattern, one hit at RVA 0x30C87C whose lea r15 at 0x30C8A6 resolves to 0x141853CE0 (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: the acting slot's address in it matched the actor pointer on every call, probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB, sim, LW, HF, MP |
| [unit-record-index-byte] | Byte +0x01 of a unit record is the unit's index, and 0xFF there marks an empty slot. | UNCERTAIN | source read: dicene/fftivc.unitcontrol and FFTMultiplayer MatchState.cs (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB, sim |
| [unit-record-job-byte] | Byte +0x03 of a unit record is the unit's job id. A Monk read 78. | UNCERTAIN | source read: dicene/fftivc.unitcontrol and FFTMultiplayer MatchState.cs (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: the Monk at slot 4, brain/probe_20261027_011757.jsonl, a log no longer kept (BB, 2026-10-27) | BB, sim, LW, MP |
| [unit-record-level-byte] | Byte +0x29 of a unit record is the unit's level. | UNCERTAIN | source read: dicene/fftivc.unitcontrol and FFTMultiplayer MatchState.cs, live there on 1.5 (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB, LW, MP |
| [unit-record-original-brave-faith] | Bytes +0x2A and +0x2C of a unit record hold its original Brave and its original Faith. | UNCERTAIN | source read: dicene/fftivc.unitcontrol and FFTMultiplayer MatchState.cs, live there on 1.5 (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB, LW, MP |
| [unit-record-in-battle-pulse] | Byte +0x2E of a unit record is what FFTMultiplayer calls the in battle pulse, a marker of being in the battle that does not hold steady. | UNCERTAIN | source read: dicene/fftivc.unitcontrol and FFTMultiplayer MatchState.cs, live there on 1.5 (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB, LW, MP |
| [unit-record-hp-words] | A unit record holds the unit's HP at +0x30 and its max HP at +0x32. | UNCERTAIN | source read: dicene/fftivc.unitcontrol and FFTMultiplayer MatchState.cs, live there on 1.5 (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB, sim, LW, MP |
| [unit-grid-xy-bytes] | Bytes +0x4F and +0x50 of a unit record are its grid X and Y. | UNCERTAIN | source read: dicene/fftivc.unitcontrol and FFTMultiplayer MatchState.cs (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: after three walks they read the requested tiles, probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB, sim, LW, MP |
| [unit-record-psx-block-shifted-eight] | The battle unit record keeps the PSX block from PA to the elevation bit 8 bytes further on: PA, MA, Speed, CT, Move, Jump, X, Y and the elevation bit each sit 8 bytes past their PSX offset, so CT, at 0x39 on PSX, would be at +0x41. Only Move and Jump are proven there, and X and Y fit the walks. | UNCERTAIN | PSX doc: FFHacktics wiki Battle Stats (BB, 2026-11-01); live tape: gear_battle_four_known_move_and_jump_pairs_at_dial_100.jsonl.gz#10, Move and Jump at the shifted offsets (BB, 2026-11-01) | BB, sim, LW |
| [unit-turn-flags-not-psx-shifted] | The PSX turn flags (turn open, moved and acted at 0x186 to 0x188) have no copy at +0x18E to +0x190 or at +0x19C to +0x19E of the battle unit record: those bytes read the same at a turn's first call and after its move on every tape. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: all 16 tapes, read in docs/research/move_jump_bytes_2026-11-01.json (BB, 2026-11-01); PSX doc: FFHacktics wiki Battle Stats (BB, 2026-11-01) | BB |
| [unit-team-bit-0x10] | Bit 0x10 of a unit's Flags2 at +0x05 marks the enemy side. Enemies read it set (Flags2 0x90 or 0x50, human bit clear), and a unit turned to the player's side, by Charm or by a skillset 0x0E Act, loses it and fights for its new side from then on. | PROVEN | live watch: slots 0, 3 and 4, and slot 1 going 0x50 to 0x40 after the Act at seq 41, probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: slot 0 going 0x90 to 0x80 when charmed, brain/probe_20261026_230258.jsonl, a log lost on 2026-10-27 (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB, sim |
| [unit-team-bits-0x30] | Bits 0x30 of Flags2 at +0x05 together hold the unit's team, and any non zero value there means not the player's team. Only bit 0x10 has been seen set live. | UNCERTAIN | source read: dicene/fftivc.unitcontrol and FFTMultiplayer MatchState.cs, the section's sources (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB, sim |
| [unit-guest-flags] | A guest unit shows bits 0x09 in Flags1 at +0x06, or bit 0x04 in Flags2 at +0x05. | UNCERTAIN | source read: dicene/fftivc.unitcontrol and FFTMultiplayer MatchState.cs, the section's sources (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB, sim |
| [unit-record-bytes-changing-at-move] | When a unit decides a Move its record changes at +0x41, +0x1B8 and +0x1B9, and after the walk +0x50, +0x1B9 and, on a three tile walk, +0x51 have changed. +0x1B8 and +0x1B9 change at every call, a counter or CT being the guess. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: per slot record diffs, probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB |
| [unit-record-bytes-changing-at-act] | When a unit decides an Act its record also changes at +0x1BB, +0x1BE, +0x1C0, +0x1CE, +0x1CF, +0x1E6 and +0x1E7, and a player unit's at +0x34, +0x1EC, +0x1ED, +0x1CA and +0x1E5 as well. | PROVEN | live watch: per slot record diffs, probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [unit-right-hand-weapon-word] | A unit's right hand weapon id is the u16 at +0x20 of its battle unit record, the records sitting at 0x141853CE0 + slot * 0x200 (the swing prep routine reaches them as the slot shifted left by 9). | PROVEN | live watch: a stub reading word [0x141853CE0 + 0x20 + rsi] at swing time drew the acting unit's own blade, Ravager id 262, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger swing-id-fallback-stub (LW, 2026-08-31); Patrick agreed 2026-08-31 | LW, BB |
| [crystal-countdown-byte] | A KO'd unit's crystal countdown is byte +0x07 of its battle unit record (LW band entry -0x15) and matches the hearts on screen, 3 then 2 and down. In a battle where KO'd units never crystallize every unit reads 255 there, in a normal battle 3. Petrify does not start it. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: dumps crystal_dump_normal and crystal_dump_firstbattle read 3 against 255 on every unit and crystal_dump_guest_2hearts read 2 with two hearts shown, the owner reading the screen, awaiting owner flip, LW ledger crystal-countdown-off-switch (LW, 2026-08-21); live watch: a petrified unit held full HP, dead bit clear and counter 3, with no byte of +0x00 to +0x17 changed, LW ledger crystal-countdown-off-switch (LW, 2026-08-21) | LW, BB |
| [crystal-countdown-off-switch] | Writing 0xFF to a KO'd unit's crystal countdown byte (+0x07 of its battle unit record) makes its hearts vanish and stops the countdown, and the game never rewrites it. Writing 2 back restores two hearts. Whether any value above 3 does the same is untested. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: on a dead guest mid countdown, both directions, crystal_dump_guest_after_write held 255 with no rewrite, tools/probes/crystal_counter_probe.py, awaiting owner flip, LW ledger crystal-countdown-off-switch (LW, 2026-08-21) | LW |
| [turn-moved-acted-flags] | A unit's battle record holds a menu open flag at +0x1B8 (1 while its move, act and wait menu is up), moved at +0x1B9 and acted at +0x1BA (LW band entry +0x19C to +0x19E). The game clears moved and acted when that unit's next turn opens, and the menu flag falling marks its turn end. +0x1BB is the PSX Ability Outcome byte (0x02 hit, 0x01 turn ended). | UNCERTAIN | live watch: Mushin banked on a still wait and spent on the strike, probes/mushin_wait_probe.py tape, owner live verified on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger turn-moved-acted-flags (LW, 2026-07-09); PSX doc: FFHacktics PSX battle struct 0x186 to 0x189, LW ledger turn-moved-acted-flags (LW, 2026-07-09) | LW, BB, sim |
| [charm-survives-break] | Charm is bit 0x20 at +0x65 of the battle unit record (LW band entry +0x49), with a second byte at +0x70 (band +0x54). Holding both at 0x20 through a hit keeps a unit charmed past the break a hit normally causes. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: held through a hit, on a build before 1.5, no owner flip named, LW ledger charm-survives-break (LW, 2026-06-08) | LW, sim |
| [poison-bit-and-timer] | Poison is bit 0x80 at +0x64 of the battle unit record (LW band entry +0x48), and its countdown is +0x66 (band +0x4A), which the game starts at 36. Setting both again after each cure keeps a unit poisoned through a two healer battle. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: a two healer battle at 1.75x rate, probes/poison_probe.py, on a build before 1.5, no owner flip named, LW ledger poison-pin-uncurable (LW, 2026-06-09) | LW, BB, sim |
| [doom-bit-and-countdown] | Doom is bit 0x01 at +0x65 of the battle unit record (LW band entry +0x49), and its countdown is +0x75 (band +0x59), which starts at 3. Lowering the countdown shows at once on the head counter. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: Eclipsebolt +3 in play and the doom research session, on a build before 1.5, no owner flip named, LW ledger doom-bytes-hasten (LW, 2026-06-09) | LW, sim |
| [unit-mp-words] | A unit's MP and max MP are u16 words at +0x34 and +0x36 of its battle unit record (LW band entry +0x18 and +0x1A). | UNCERTAIN | live watch: MP visibly restored on screen, gated per battle by a layout check, on a build before 1.5, no owner flip named, LW ledger mp-pair-on-band (LW, 2026-06-10) | LW, BB, sim |
| [inb-flag-pulses] | The in battle byte at +0x2E of the battle unit record (LW band entry +0x12) pulses during a battle, with about half the live enemies reading 0 at any instant, so a live unit filter cannot rest on it. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: half the live enemies read 0 at any instant, on a build before 1.5, no owner flip named, LW ledger inb-flag-pulses (LW, 2026-06-09) | LW, BB |
| [unit-ct-speed-bytes] | A unit's CT is byte +0x41 and its Speed byte +0x40 of its battle unit record: the CT the game's own turn list showed on four units was the +0x41 a turn file read one enemy turn earlier plus one tick of the +0x40 Speed of 13. Speed holds steady per unit, 13 for Archers and the player's units and 9 for two job 126 monsters, while CT climbs between a unit's turns. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner's screen against the live turn file, map 80 turn 10 (BB, 2026-11-03); live tape: first_driver_battle_stub_then_opus_plays_the_enemy#7 to #32668, the 174 whole board lines (BB, 2026-11-01); source read: FFTLivingWeapons LivingWeapon/Offsets.cs, Speed +0x40 and CT +0x41, proven live there on this build (BB, 2026-11-01); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |
| [status-layer-bit-order] | A unit's statuses are the five bytes +0x61 to +0x65 of its battle unit record, read most significant bit first with the bit index as the status id: 2 Dead, 3 Undead, 4 Charging, 15 Treasure, 17 Float, 18 Reraise, 24 Poison, 25 Regen, 26 Protect, 27 Shell, 28 Haste, 34 Charm, 38 Reflect, 39 Doom. | UNCERTAIN | source read: FFTLivingWeapons LivingWeapon/Offsets.cs, its composed status layers, proven live there on this build (BB, 2026-11-01) | BB |
| [unit-array-mirrored-per-frame] | The battle unit array at 0x141853CE0 is a mirror the game refreshes every frame: a byte loop at 0x14038B21D and 0x14038B25C copies a source record, held in rcx about 0x55E61 bytes below the mirror, field by field into it, which is how writes the game makes elsewhere reach the array. An enemy's turn starts in that source and the mirror follows. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-11-01_turn_start.json, the writers 0x14038B220 and 0x14038B25F, 43 to 59 hits per watch while a turn was held (BB, 2026-11-01) | BB |
| [unit-2e-set-after-first-turn] | Byte +0x2E of a unit's battle record reads 0 on every unit until its first turn and 1 after it, across a whole battle's board lines, so it is neither an in battle flag nor an acting flag. An older build's reading has it pulsing, see [inb-flag-pulses]. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: first_driver_battle_stub_then_opus_plays_the_enemy#7 to #32668, the 174 whole board lines (BB, 2026-11-01) | BB |
| [crystal-body-flags2-0x90] | A downed enemy that has turned to a crystal reads Flags2 0x90 at +0x05, where plain downed bodies read 0x50, and its slot goes empty once a unit takes the crystal. | [auto-battle-mode-byte] | Byte +0x1EC of a unit's battle unit record switches the game's own auto-battle for that unit, 0 off and 0x0C on. Other values are unprobed. | PROVEN | live watch: no log, the owner and a script wrote 0x0C at Ramza's +0x1EC and the Auto tag showed, his +0x05 staying 0x0B (BB, 2026-10-29); live watch: brain/probe_20261029_041250.jsonl #109 and #110, his next turn on auto with the byte still 0x0C (BB, 2026-10-29); live watch: brain/probe_20261029_055737.jsonl #19, #20 and #22, the menu's own off write, a log no longer kept (BB, 2026-10-29); source read: LW ledger auto-battle-mode-byte, an owner Cheat Engine session, uncertain there (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [unit-place-three-layers] | The game keeps a unit's place in three layers apart. The battle unit record's tile is X +0x4F and Y +0x50 with the upper level as bit 0x80 of +0x51. The node's world point is u16 X at +0x4C and Y at +0x50, each 28 times the tile plus 14, with Z at +0x4E as minus 12 times the tile's height, one height more under Float. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner's screen and tools/force.py on map 80, units written to new tiles in all three layers stood, were listed and were hit there, no log (BB, 2026-11-03); source read: FFTLivingWeapons' live ledger of 2026-07-10 (BB, 2026-11-03); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |

| [status-two-layers-layout] | A unit's statuses sit in two layers of its battle unit record, the composed set at +0x61 to +0x65 (LW band +0x45 to +0x49) and the inflicted registry at +0x1EF to +0x1F3 (band +0x1D3 to +0x1D7), one bit per StatusEffectData id, most significant bit first. Id 0 is +0x61 bit 0x80, and Dead 0x20 and Charging 0x08 share that byte. | PROVEN | live watch: tools/probes/provoke_mark_probe.py saw a cast set composed 00 00 40 00 00 to 80 00 40 00 00 and inflicted 00 00 00 00 00 to 80 00 00 00 00 within one 33 ms sample, and a mid charge unit read 0x88, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger status-removal-one-sided (LW, 2026-07-22); live watch: the shipping mod's mask scoped clear of both layers scrubbed a stray mark within a tick in the regression pass, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger status-removal-one-sided (LW, 2026-08-12); Patrick agreed 2026-08-12 | LW |
| [maxhp-write-leaves-current-hp] | Raising a unit's max HP mid battle leaves its current HP where it was: Ramza at 624 of 624, held at 724, showed 624 of 724 on his card, the bar drew sanely, and the battle ended and saved normally. | PROVEN | live watch: tools/probes/lw316_lane_probes.py maxhp_hold on Ramza, 624 to 724, current HP untouched by the game, battle end save clean 13:17:41, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger maxhp-hold-attribution-safe (LW, 2026-08-25); Patrick agreed 2026-08-25 | LW |
| [unit-facing-low-bits] | A unit's facing is the low 2 bits of +0x51 of its battle unit record (LW band +0x35), the byte whose bit 7 is its terrain level: 0 S, 1 W, 2 N, 3 E, looking toward (0,-1), (-1,0), (0,+1) and (+1,0), so the tile behind it is its own tile minus that step. It follows the Wait facing wheel live, before the confirm. | PROVEN | live watch: tools/probes/facing_probe.py watch saw seat 24 step 0x01 W to 0x02 N on Ramza's Wait wheel before confirm, node +0x2C mirroring, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger facing-byte-band-0x35 (LW, 2026-07-28); live watch: the facing W plant barred (6,10) directly behind a wielder at (5,10), owner eyewitness, Bulwark regression pass, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger facing-byte-band-0x35 (LW, 2026-08-12); source read: FFTHandsFree FacingByteDecoder.cs and its project_facing_byte_s30 memory, owner checked 4 units by eye, LW ledger facing-byte-band-0x35 (LW, 2026-04-17); source read: FFTLivingWeapons docs/LIVE_LEDGER.md facing-byte-band-0x35, owner flipped there 2026-08-12 (BB, 2026-11-03); PSX doc: FFHacktics wiki Facing Evade Calculation (BB, 2026-11-03); Patrick agreed 2026-08-12 | LW, BB |
| [facing-south-is-plus-y] | Facing south, value 0, means the unit looks toward +y on the battle grid, as FFTHandsFree's delta note reads. | CONTRADICTED | live watch: a facing N plant barred (x,y+1) and drew in front of the unit, and enemies at low y read N while facing players at high y, so south looks toward y-1, tools/probes/facing_probe.py, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger facing-byte-band-0x35 (LW, 2026-07-28) | LW |
| [action-record-pending-stamp] | A unit's action record, +0x1A0 of its battle unit record (LW band +0x184), names the action it is performing: its byte +0x0A reads 5 and its u16 at +0x02 holds the ability id, 0 for the basic Attack. The stamp is set before damage lands, lasts past the kill, and comes back within about a second after a reaction overwrites it. | PROVEN | live watch: tools/probes/arec_watch.py at 20 Hz over an owner driven 4 beat battle, a fatal Attack held 0 and a fatal Rend Weapon held 141 from the blow to the credit line 0.7 s later, and Auto-Potion overwrote the stamp three times with it coming back each time, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger basic-attack-discriminator (LW, 2026-08-12); live watch: in the arming pass the ability control beat read kind 5 ability 102 and five basic Attack poaches read true with no false fire, LW ledger basic-attack-discriminator (LW, 2026-08-12); Patrick agreed 2026-08-12 | LW |
| [targetless-attack-action-words] | A basic Attack at an empty tile writes the Attack ability 0x1B9 to +0x1A2 and effect 0xF2 to +0x1A8 of the attacker's battle unit record as it swings, and the swing's animation page follows that ability id: the same two words held from outside on another unit gave a spell cast pose. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: read only tapes and reversible holds with the owner at the controls, vanilla's targetless swing receiving both words and the Ravager's never, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger empty-tile-hand-read-bounds (LW, 2026-08-31) | LW |

| [battle-mode-byte-is-input-mode] | The byte at 0x1409069A0 that mods call the battle mode is an input mode, not a box flag: it read 3 with the battle menus up and 1 while either crystal Yes/No box waited. Its one writer is a task that the set input mode routine 0x140234A94 (31 call sites) registers. Whether mode 1 also shows on the move tile screen is unread. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: six read-only snapshots of the exe's .data, run1 to run3, down-after, up-moveconfirm and up-restoreconfirm, kept in a session scratchpad (BB, 2026-10-29); Ghidra read: offline xref of 0x1409069A0 (BB, 2026-10-29) | BB |
| [map-id-written-at-battle-load] | The game writes the battle map id at 0x140784478 only as a battle loads: 0x1402517C2 wrote it (from r14d) once as the battle loaded for its formation screen and once as a suspended battle resumed, each right after the load call 0x140251584 with the same number. Before each, 0x14033D78A wrote the fill 0x77777777 there twice. Nothing wrote it during play. A retry and a return to the world map are unwatched. | PROVEN | live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-10-29.jsonl, windows launch2_formation and launch3_resume_auto_crystal (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [map-id-reset-routine] | A map reset routine, 0x14033D6B0 to 0x14033D7E3, writes the fill 0x77777777 over the map id (the store at 0x14033D78A) after two calls to 0x1405CA430, so the next map load always takes its write path. It ran before each battle load and once about 40 s before the first with no load after. Four direct callers: 0x140234360, 0x1402D3FA0, 0x1402F40C4 and 0x1402F41D0. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: map reset 0x14033D6B0 to 0x14033D7E3 (BB, 2026-10-29); live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-10-29.jsonl, the fill writes (BB, 2026-10-29) | BB |
| [map-loader-skips-same-map] | The map loader 0x1402516BC to 0x140252213 takes the map number in ecx and a load kind in edx, 0x73, 0x75 or 0x99, any other kind skipping the load. When the map asked for equals the map id at 0x140784478 it skips both the load and the write; otherwise it loads through 0x140251584 and writes the id at 0x1402517C2. Ten direct calls reach it from 0x140202930, 0x14020D730, 0x14023A0B4, 0x1403044EC and 0x14030476C. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: map loader 0x1402516BC to 0x140252213 (BB, 2026-10-29) | BB |
| [battle-render-node-list] | The game keeps its battle units' render nodes on a list at 0x140D3A410: each node's first eight bytes point to the next node and the eight at +0x148 to the battle unit record it draws. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner's screen and tools/force.py on map 80, nodes found this way taking the written place, no log (BB, 2026-11-03); source read: FFTLivingWeapons' live ledger of 2026-07-10 (BB, 2026-11-03); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |
| [script-variables-word-array] | The game's script variables 0x00 to 0x7F are 4 byte words at [0x1407830D8] + index * 4. Among them 0x27 is the story scenario, 0x32 the encounter and 0x35 a row of the table at [0x1408ECA58]. The world map routine copies the map word 0x140D4C944 into 0x33 at 0x14024672A on its way out, the map picker reads 0x33 at 0x14020E818, and the picker tests 0x27 against a range, a story battle lead. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: the Fight routine 0x140248254 and the world map routine 0x140246078 (BB, 2026-11-03); live watch: trap hits, docs/research/live_traps_2026-11-03_keyless_setup.jsonl (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [script-var-0x32-sets-tiles-enemies] | Script variable 0x32, which Fight fills from 0x140D494C8, likely sets a battle's starting tiles and enemies. Mandalia's tiles came right once 0x32 and 0x35 were written together, and a keyless Dorter start that left 0x32 at a stale 103 put the cursor on 3,0, as in the Mandalia crash, fielded no enemies and dealt other tiles. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: the owner's screen at keyless starts on Mandalia Plain and Dorter, no log named (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [script-var-0x35-row-table] | Script variable 0x35 picks a 12 byte row of the table at [0x1408ECA58], 768 rows over 118 maps, with a map id at +9 and its own number at +10. It does not set tiles or enemies, which stayed the same on Dorter between rows 62 and 604, and alone it does not fix the light: four keyless starts on one map with row 554 and encounter 166 lit differently each time. Variable 0x23, which the game re-rolls, is the next lead. | UNCERTAIN | game table: the rows at [0x1408ECA58] (BB, 2026-11-03); live watch: Dorter started on rows 62 and 604, and real Orbonne drawn with row 62 left over, no log named (BB, 2026-11-03); live watch: screens of four keyless runs, no log named (BB, 2026-11-04) | BB |
| [scene-state-word-values] | The word at 0x140C6B1CC is the game's scene state. Values seen: 0 at the title, 3 a clean world map, 4 a unit's menu open, 17 a battle frozen after teleports, 19 the facing prompt after Wait, 21 a box waiting on a key, 32 the target prompt, 52 a battle under way, 59 (0x3B) the way onto the world map, 75 a Continue load, 83 the formation screen and 85 (0x55) Game Over. A commence steps it 83, 84, 52, 58, 52, and a leave from Game Over passes 80 and 95 to 59. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: reads at formation and commence, no log named (BB, 2026-11-03); live watch: reads and video at Game Over and the leaves, F:\FFTRec\kept\leave (BB, 2026-11-04); live watch: one trapped Continue, docs/research/title_continue_2026-11-04.jsonl (BB, 2026-11-04); live watch: the mod's log at the menu prompts, sheets in F:\FFTRec\sheets (BB, 2026-11-05) | BB |
| [picture-node-list-layout] | Each unit on the field has a picture node in a list: +0x08 is its id byte, +0x88 and +0x8C its tile twice, +0x90 the tile its last walk left, +0x12C a mode byte and +0x148 the record it draws. The low byte of 0x140CF873C names the acting node's id. Half a second into the first turn the acting unit's node had moved both copies, to 5,2 and 5,3 from a start on 5,1, while its record kept its own tile. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: reads, cast_try_trim_2.log and cast_try_full_2.log in F:\FFTRec\kept\arena\learning_build (BB, 2026-11-05) | BB |

| [hiding-all-enemies-wins-battle] | The victory check counts living enemies over the same list the gate byte takes a unit off: hiding every enemy at once ends the battle in a win. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner hid every enemy at once and the battle ended in a win, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger hide-reveal-ghost-statue (LW, 2026-07-22); proven in LW 2026-07-22 | LW |
| [despawn-unit-mode2] | Any unit can be removed mid battle, sprite and list entry included, by putting its render node into removal mode ([unit-presence-fields]): the game's node sweeper finishes the job on the next unpaused frame, the path crystallization takes, and the removal is one way. | PROVEN | live watch: a hovered enemy Knight vanished, unit, sprite and list entry, on unpause, every predicted side effect byte reading as decoded, BodyDoubleSpike Ctrl+F5, owner live, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger despawn-unit-mode2 (LW, 2026-07-10); live watch: the owner's screen and the reads, five goblins with slots 5 and 4 removed, cast_try_trim_2.log in F:\FFTRec\kept\arena\learning_build (BB, 2026-11-05); source read: LW ledger despawn-unit-mode2, proven on LW's build, where the sweeper test sits 0x78 higher (BB, 2026-11-05); Patrick agreed 2026-07-21; proven in BB 2026-11-05 | LW, BB |
| [unit-presence-fields] | A unit's presence spans its record's +0x01 (model id, 0xFF out of play) and +0x1B5 (1 present, 0x80 removed) and its render node's flag word +0x12C. Writing (old AND NOT 0x30) OR 0x20 there asks for removal, which the sweeper 0x14026E20C completes by setting both record bytes, marking the word done (0x30), freeing the node's pool slot and unlinking it from 0x140D3A410. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: every side effect byte read as predicted on a despawn, BodyDoubleSpike, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger despawn-unit-mode2 (LW, 2026-07-10); live watch: a despawned Knight returned after its node was revived, +0x1B5 set to 1 and +0x01 set to its model last, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger resurrect-despawned-unit (LW, 2026-07-10); Ghidra read: the sweeper and the leaf teardown 0x14026E16C decoded, and the old cold call target 0x14023BFB0 found mid instruction inside the event interpreter 0x14023A8E0, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger despawn-unit-mode2 (LW, 2026-07-10) | LW |
| [resurrect-despawned-unit] | A despawned unit's record and render node bytes survive the removal, so it can be brought back mid battle by data writes alone, re-enrolled with the AI before it is made visible, then walked to and hit for real damage with the battle going on. | PROVEN | live watch: a despawned enemy Knight was brought back, walked to and punched for real damage, owner live, unit-despawn-resurrect recipe memo, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger resurrect-despawned-unit (LW, 2026-07-10); Patrick agreed 2026-07-21 | LW |
| [spawn-ai-unit-donor-duplication] | A real AI unit can be added mid battle by duplicating a live one: its record copied to a free slot and tile, its AI registry object cloned, a render node built at the new tile and its AI roster index set. The clone draws, is named, can be controlled, takes real AI turns, and lasts only that battle. | PROVEN | live watch: the clone descended from the sky, took a real AI turn once its roster index went 0xFF to 0x08 and fought a full battle with no crash, BodyDoubleSpike Canary 8 and 9, owner live, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger spawn-ai-unit-donor-duplication (LW, 2026-07-10); Patrick agreed 2026-07-21 | LW |
| [ai-roster-index-gates-ai-turns] | Each slot's AI roster index byte at 0x141873038 + slot holds 0 to 7 for real units. While any occupied slot reads 0x10 or more (0xFF when unenrolled), the AI turn code at 0x151086A53 snapshots no subject, the subject pointer 0x141872EA0 stays null, and the facing code at 0x150E74A5D dereferences it and crashes the game. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: an unindexed clone crashed auto battle until its byte was set, BodyDoubleSpike, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger spawn-ai-unit-donor-duplication (LW, 2026-07-10); Ghidra read: the early gate cmp [0x141873038+slot],0x10 with jae to 0x151086DA0, and movzx ecx,[rax+0x51] at 0x150E74A5D, byte cited by three decode workflows, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger spawn-ai-unit-donor-duplication (LW, 2026-07-10) | LW |
| [ai-registry-object-pool] | The battle's AI registry objects sit in a pool at 0x1437415A0, 0x280 bytes each, keyed by the unit's slot in the word at +0x2C, with their pointers listed at 0x141800F50 and the count kept in the word at 0x1437414A8 and the byte at 0x140D407BB. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: a donor's object cloned, re-keyed and appended this way gave a clone that took real AI turns, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger spawn-ai-unit-donor-duplication (LW, 2026-07-10); live watch: the same re-enroll brought a despawned unit back, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger resurrect-despawned-unit (LW, 2026-07-10) | LW |

### Turns and battle flow

| Id | Fact | Mark | Evidence | Leaned on by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [ai-sometimes-acts-before-moving] | The game's own AI sometimes acts before it moves, so a turn can run Act, then Move, then EndTurn. The phase order is not fixed. | PROVEN | live watch: no log, slot 2 on turn 36 of the second battle of 2026-10-26 evening (BB, 2026-10-26); live tape: first_live_battle_hold_fold_and_handbrake#4773 (BB, 2026-10-27); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB, sim |
| [move-then-act-turn-skips-endturn] | A turn that moves and then acts ends after the Act with no EndTurn request, its facing coming from the attack. A turn that acts first still gets an EndTurn after its Move. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: slot 3's attack turn, where a facing write armed for EndTurn never fired, probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: turn 4, nothing more asked after the Act, brain/probe_20261026_230258.jsonl, a log lost on 2026-10-27 (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB, sim |
| [battle-lost-when-ramza-is-out] | The battle is lost once Ramza is out for good, even with another player unit standing. Game over came when his KO counter ran out with slot 17 still at 392 HP, and again after a Blaster petrified him. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: slot 16, Ramza, at 0 HP from seq 14, probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); live tape: s1_conversions_hand_mode_araguay_woods_ending_in_petrify#444 (BB, 2026-10-28) | BB, sim |
| [ai-played-turn-keeps-exp-jp] | A player unit whose turns the game's AI plays, its human bit cleared at battle entry, still gains EXP and JP exactly as a hand played turn does. | PROVEN | live watch: no log, the owner's Archer in the third battle of 2026-10-26 evening (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [retried-battle-opens-zeroed-request] | When a lost battle is retried, the routine's first call in the new attempt brings an all zero request, with the units on new tiles, a fallen unit back at full HP and the unit table pointer moved. | PROVEN | live tape: s1_conversions_hand_mode_araguay_woods_ending_in_petrify#446 (BB, 2026-10-28); live watch: no log, the owner retried after the game over (BB, 2026-10-28); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [stale-act-on-retry-stalls-battle] | Answering the first call of a retried battle with an Act left over from the lost attempt left the game stopped at that turn, window responsive, with no further calls until a relaunch. Whether the stale Act caused it is open. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: s1_conversions_hand_mode_araguay_woods_ending_in_petrify#446 and no line after it (BB, 2026-10-28) | BB |
| [ct-slam-extra-turn] | Writing 100 into a unit's CT byte, +0x41 of its battle unit record (LW band entry +0x25), gives that unit the next turn. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: Zwill v8 played live, probes/ct_probe.py found the byte, on a build before 1.5, no owner flip named, LW ledger ct-slam-extra-turn (LW, 2026-06-09) | LW, BB, sim |
| [enemy-turn-byte-rises-and-falls] | On an enemy unit the byte at +0x25 of its battle unit record (LW band entry +0x09) climbs to 90 or more and drops below 70 once per turn it takes, so each rise and fall counts one turn. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: Maim and CharmLock victim windows in play, on a build before 1.5, no owner flip named, LW ledger victim-turn-counting (LW, 2026-06-09) | LW |
| [poison-edge-and-acted-flag-skew] | When an attack poisons a unit, the target's poison bit and the attacker's acted flag can change up to about 2 seconds apart, in either order. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: four open windows and zero latches before a grace window, a chocobo cleansing the held poison, on a build before 1.5, no owner flip named, LW ledger plague-grace-window (LW, 2026-06-10) | LW |
| [next-unit-computable] | The game's turn order follows each unit's CT and Speed: a forecast from +0x41 and +0x40 named the same next five units, in the same order, as the game's own turn list, five being as deep as the screen showed. Only that head of the order is known to hold, since FFTLivingWeapons' forecast went wrong 2 to 6 turns out. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner's screen against the live turn file, map 80 turn 10 (BB, 2026-11-03); source read: LW ledger next-unit-computable, the next unit right 15 of 15 times across two sessions (BB, 2026-11-01); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |
| [ct-paid-at-turn-start] | A unit pays about 100 CT as its turn opens: its +0x41 reads about 100 lower on its own turn's board lines than on the last line before them, and a held turn read CT 0 with the acting flag 1. No separate drop at the open has been caught on the record itself. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: first_driver_battle_stub_then_opus_plays_the_enemy#7 to #32668, the 174 whole board lines (BB, 2026-11-01); source read: LW ledger ct-paid-at-turn-start, proven live there on this build (BB, 2026-11-01); live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-11-01_turn_start.json, r_ct and e7_ct (BB, 2026-11-01) | BB |
| [ct-pay-no-immediate-subtract] | No instruction in the exe's .text subtracts the immediate 100 from a byte at +0x41 of any base register, so the CT payment at a turn's open goes through a register and only a write trap on the acting unit's +0x41 can name its writer. | UNCERTAIN | exe scan: .text byte scan with the .pdata function bounds, docs/research/turn_start_scan_2026-11-01.json (BB, 2026-11-01) | BB |
| [acting-flag-immediate-writers] | Only two instructions write the acting flag +0x1B8 by immediate, and both clear it: 0x140275CEC inside CopyUnitToBattleUnit (RVA 0x275CA4) and 0x1403107CD inside the 72 byte function 0x1403107A4, which never fired at a turn's end. None sets the flag that way. | UNCERTAIN | exe scan: docs/research/turn_start_scan_2026-11-01.json (BB, 2026-11-01); live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-11-01_turn_start.json, 0x1403107A4 never fired (BB, 2026-11-01) | BB |
| [ct-clock-tick-writer] | The game's clock raises CT from the function 0x14030F030: a callee at 0x14030F9A0, returning to 0x14030F40E, wrote Ramza's CT 31 times between the first enemy's turn end and his own turn open, and the write just before 0x14030F4AD touched every unit's CT 16 times in the second before slot 7's turn opened. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-11-01_turn_start.json, r_ct and e7_ct (BB, 2026-11-01) | BB |
| [human-turn-open-sets-acting-flag] | A turn the player plays opens in the function 0x14038A468 (unwind entry to 0x14038AA66), whose instruction at 0x14038A6BF writes 1 into the acting flag +0x1B8 of the record it picks by slot. An enemy's turn opened with no write from it, its flag reaching the array through the per frame copy. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-11-01_turn_start.json, r_flag, 30 hits in the second Ramza's turn opened (BB, 2026-11-01) | BB |
| [turn-end-routine-clears-acting-flag] | A leaf routine at 0x140310650, with no unwind entry, runs at every turn's end, among other calls, with the unit's slot in ecx: it indexes the battle unit array, checks the record is live and acting, and clears the acting flag +0x1B8 at 0x1403106D0. | PROVEN | live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-11-01_turn_start.json, e_flag, e_ct, r_flag, r_ct and e7, one hit each at a turn's end (BB, 2026-11-01); live tape: turn_end_leaf_called_per_tile_by_the_ai_on_auto_turns, a hook on its entry fired at every turn's end (BB, 2026-11-01); live tape: hand_played_turn_calls_the_leaf_twice_at_its_end, the same (BB, 2026-11-01); proven in BB 2026-11-01 | BB |
| [turn-end-ct-refund] | At a turn's end the game gives CT back: the turn end routine writes at 0x1403106F8 the old CT plus 20 if the unit did not move plus 20 if it did not act, capped at 60, reading the moved and acted state from +0x61, +0x65, +0x1B9, +0x1BA and +0x1BC. Ramza, who moved and acted, kept CT 10. | PROVEN | live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-11-01_turn_start.json, e_ct and r_ct at turn ends (BB, 2026-11-01); live tape: turn_end_leaf_called_per_tile_by_the_ai_on_auto_turns, the CT read at every turn's end (BB, 2026-11-01); live tape: hand_played_turn_calls_the_leaf_twice_at_its_end, the same (BB, 2026-11-01); proven in BB 2026-11-01 | BB |
| [turn-end-routine-runs-twice] | The turn end routine 0x140310650 runs twice at a turn's end. On a turn that acts, the first call comes as the act is chosen, before its damage reaches the target's record, and the second after it; on a turn that only moves both calls see the same board. The pair came 0.7 to 8.6 s apart. A hand-played turn never calls it earlier, and an AI-played turn makes the pair after the battle routine's second call. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: hand_played_turn_calls_the_leaf_twice_at_its_end, the hand-played turns of slots 16 and 17 and the AI pairs (BB, 2026-11-01); live tape: turn_end_leaf_called_per_tile_by_the_ai_on_auto_turns, the AI pairs (BB, 2026-11-01); live tape: turn_end_filter_live_hand_played_pairs_and_think_ahead, six hand-played turns (BB, 2026-11-01) | BB |
| [party-unit-builder-routine] | The game builds each deployed party unit straight in its unit table slot with the routine at RVA 0x275B84, reached from the deploy loop at 0x270CE4 through a stub at 0x275C70 that looks up the roster entry. The routine zeroes +0x1EC and then calls CopyJobEffectsToUnit (RVA 0x276C64) on the slot, at a fresh battle and at a new battle later in the same launch. | PROVEN | live tape: auto_battle_player_side_stamped_at_setup_opus_plays_the_enemy#43, #45, #46 and #48 (BB, 2026-10-29); live watch: brain/probe_20261029_055737.jsonl #14 and #15, a second battle in the same launch, a log no longer kept (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [auto-byte-written-after-build-survives] | A 0x0C written into a party unit's +0x1EC right after CopyJobEffectsToUnit returns at deployment survives to the battle's first turn: slots 16 and 20 were written before the first routine call and Ramza's first turn played on auto with no menu. | PROVEN | live tape: auto_battle_player_side_stamped_at_setup_opus_plays_the_enemy#43 to #48 (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [enemy-unit-builder-routine] | The game builds enemy units with CopyUnitToBattleUnit (RVA 0x275CA4), whose caller at 0x27128C fills slots below 16. In one battle it ran on enemy slots 0 to 10 only, never on a player slot. | PROVEN | live tape: auto_battle_player_side_stamped_at_setup_opus_plays_the_enemy#20 to #41 (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [copyjobeffects-runs-on-stack-records] | At battle load CopyJobEffectsToUnit also runs on unit records outside the unit table, on the stack, called by other builders. | PROVEN | live watch: brain/probe_20261029_045726.jsonl notes #12 and #13, a log no longer kept (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [set-status-all-runs-mid-battle] | The game runs set_status_all on unit records mid battle: on enemy slots, and on a player slot within seconds of its auto-battle being turned off in the battle menu. It did not run on the player's slot before the first player turn. | PROVEN | live watch: brain/probe_20261029_045726.jsonl note #15, a log no longer kept (BB, 2026-10-29); live tape: auto_battle_player_side_stamped_at_setup_opus_plays_the_enemy#446 on (BB, 2026-10-29); live watch: brain/probe_20261029_055737.jsonl #19, #20 and #22, slot 16 at 1364 s and 1371 s, a log no longer kept (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [auto-byte-not-carried-between-battles] | A unit's auto-battle byte +0x1EC does not carry from one battle to the next: Ramza's read 0x0C at the end of one battle and 0x00 at the start of the next. | PROVEN | live watch: no log, the owner and a script (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |

| [worldmap-flag-write-starts-battle] | From the world map, with no key press and no encounter roll, writing the word at 0x141D32E14 with bit 0 cleared and bit 15 (0x8000) set takes the game straight to the formation screen, on the map the scene value at 0x140D3A838 already holds. The word read 0x4000003 before and 0x4008000 after. The same write from the Enemy encountered box also reaches formation, but leaves the box drawn on top until the next return to the world map. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner's screen and two writes, map 80, Araguay Woods, docs/research/live_traps_2026-11-03_battle_start.jsonl (BB, 2026-11-03); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |
| [worldmap-loop-exit-bits] | The world map routine 0x140246078 keeps looping while bit 0 of the word at 0x141D32E14 is set, and on leaving returns 1, a battle, when bit 15 is set, so bit 15 alone does nothing. The scene loop stores the result at 0x141853C08 and calls the dispatcher 0x140210AF0, which runs the map picker 0x14020E578 and the encounter setup 0x1401EE9B4. Bit 0 reads 0 on the boot screens and 1 once a save is up. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: the world map routine 0x140246078 and the scene loop (BB, 2026-11-03); live watch: four trap runs and a bit 15 only write that changed nothing, no log named (BB, 2026-11-03); live watch: one run's reads from launch, bit 0 by screen, no log named (BB, 2026-11-04) | BB |
| [battle-node-enter-rolls-encounter] | Enter on a world map battle node calls 0x140248BE4, which rolls the encounter (it rolled 1 and left map id 0x50 in rcx), then 0x1402477C8(3, 0x67), which opens the Enemy encountered box. Nothing else traced moves until Fight. Not traced: a save load, a first battle after boot, and whether the roster comes from the last roll. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: trap runs at Araguay Woods, no log named (BB, 2026-11-03); Ghidra read: 0x140248BE4 and 0x1402477C8 (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |

| [map-only-start-faults-at-place] | A battle started from outside with only the map word and the battle start write can fault when the first unit is placed on formation, at 0x1401F330F, which reads [rbx+0x18]+0x148 with [rbx+0x18] empty. Map 56 with variable 0x32 at 387 and 0x27 at 4 also played Orbonne's opening scene and then its battle, with black ground. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner's screen and the reads, docs/research/live_traps_2026-11-05_story_start.jsonl (BB, 2026-11-05); live watch: the owner's screen and one write from his real save, nothing saved, no log named (BB, 2026-11-03); proven in BB 2026-11-05; proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |
| [formation-place-node-lookup] | Placing a unit on formation fills its slot at 0x1401F2F5F from 0x140260ABC, which walks the list at 0x140D3A410 for the node whose record +0x1BC equals the unit's index. A unit whose start tile fails the check 0x140271178 gets no node, the lookup returns 0 and 0x1401F330F faults. In the Mandalia crash the list held 5 nodes, for units 0, 3, 5, 7 and 11, none for unit 16, where a normal start gave it node 0x140D33A50. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: trap hits at 0x1401F330F and on RBX+0x18, no log named (BB, 2026-11-03); Ghidra read: 0x1401F2F5F, 0x140260ABC and 0x140271178 (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [formation-step-word-commences] | Writing 0x190 to the step word at +0x30 of the formation object, with a unit placed and no key press, commences the battle: the steps ran up to 0x19A, the state value at 0x140C6B1CC went 84, 52, 58, 52 as in a real commence, and the battle played, with the formation panels left drawn on top. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner's screen and one write, Mandalia Plain with Ramza placed by hand, no log named (BB, 2026-11-03); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |
| [formation-object-yes-path] | The formation object has vtable 0x140713F48, and 0x1401F008C runs its step word, which sits at 0x64 while it waits for input. A real Yes runs from 0x1401F1E12: it stamps +0x191 on the placed player units, calls the camera routine 0x1400FB3B0, sets the step to 0x190 at 0x1401F1E72, then calls 0x1401EDD50 and 0x1401D861C. The Space path at 0x1401F1FF0 makes the roster panel call, calls 0x14021060C and 0x1400F1C5C and writes 3 to +0x768. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: trap hits, no log named (BB, 2026-11-03); Ghidra read: 0x1401F008C and the Yes path from 0x1401F1E12 (BB, 2026-11-03); Ghidra read: the Space path at 0x1401F1FF0 (BB, 2026-11-04) | BB |
| [formation-place-routine] | 0x1401F3128 places the formation object's current unit, with 1 in its second argument on the key path, and uses the cursor only to look for a unit already there. The screen reads input only while its +0x60 and +0xBB0 and the bytes at 0x140C6B307 and 0x140CE3232 are all 0. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: 0x1401F3128 and 0x1401F2E4C (BB, 2026-11-03); live watch: one trap hit each, Ramza landing on 3,0 with the cursor on 0,2, no log named (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [scenario-table-layout] | The scenario table at 0x1408ECAB0 has 24 byte rows: number at +0, map at +2, ENTD at +7, squad at +9, next scenario at +0x12 and its action at +0x14. 206 rows carry a byte at +0x16 counting up through the game, 1 for scenario 3 at Orbonne, 3 for 9 at Gariland, 10 for 33 at Dorter Slums, and that row is the first of its battle's chain, the number to start from. 0x1402FB7C8 is the PSX Load Scenario Conditionals routine. | UNCERTAIN | game table: one live read of the scenario table at 0x1408ECAB0 (BB, 2026-11-05); Ghidra read: 0x1402FB7C8 (BB, 2026-11-05); PSX doc: FFHacktics wiki ATTACK.OUT Load Scenario Conditionals (BB, 2026-11-05) | BB |
