### Combat rules

| Id | Fact | Mark | Evidence | Leaned on by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [act-target-range-not-checked] | The game does not check an Act's target against the ability's range: a Knight's basic attack written at Ramza four tiles away hit him as if adjacent, though the range highlight showed only the adjacent tiles. | PROVEN | live watch: no log, slot 2 at (9,5) against Ramza at (7,7) in the second battle of 2026-10-26 evening (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [self-centred-act-ignores-target-tile] | A self centred area ability fires around its user whatever target tile the Act names: ability 321 written with target (1,1) still hit both player units beside its user, and one countered. | PROVEN | live watch: turn 4 and the owner's view, brain/probe_20261026_230258.jsonl, a log lost on 2026-10-27 (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [knight-sword-damage-uses-brave] | A knight sword's basic attack scales with the wielder's Brave: PA x WP x Brave/100, times zodiac compatibility. PA 21, WP 15 and Brave 69 at 125 percent compatibility dealt 272 to a chocobo. | PROVEN | live watch: id 261 cloned to the Chaos Blade's accessors swung for 272 on a chocobo on the 1.5.2 Denuvo build, screenshots tools/probes/lw346_moonblade_swing_blade_1.png and _2.png, LW ledger capbreak-swing-art-via-accessor-clones (LW, 2026-08-27); Patrick agreed 2026-08-27 | LW |
| [battle-reach-not-from-weapon-row] | A weapon's battle reach is not the range byte at [0] of the weapon stats row the weapon stat accessor returns: a row reading range 1 still reached 2 tiles, the range of its accessors' donor and of every vanilla Lunging category. Which of those two decides is unresolved. | PROVEN | live watch: re-test 7, the Terrastaff at id 262 with an authored range of 1 targeted 2 tiles, donor 108 Ironreed Pole, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger extended-reach-from-donor-not-authored-row (LW, 2026-08-30); Patrick agreed 2026-08-31 | LW |
| [spin-punch-reach-by-height] | Spin Punch, the Black Goblin's attack, reaches a tile half a height step up but not 1.5 or 2 up: at 0.5 up the tile lit and the hit landed, while at 1.5 and 2 up no tile lit at all, so the target is out of reach, not a miss. | PROVEN | live watch: Patrick's own eyes, an Araguay practice battle staged by the BB simulator 19:50 to 20:21, hand_check_practice_1948.log in F:\FFTRec\kept\arena\three_jobs_2026-11-12 (BB, 2026-11-12); live tape: 234 of 234 recorded Spin Punch hits within 1 height (BB, 2026-11-12); Patrick agreed 2026-11-12 | BB, sim |
| [spin-punch-113-on-chocobo] | Spin Punch did 113 damage to a chocobo, 467 to 354 HP. | PROVEN | live watch: Patrick's own eyes, the second Araguay practice battle staged by the BB simulator, hand_check_practice_1958.log at 20:12:54 in F:\FFTRec\kept\arena\three_jobs_2026-11-12 (BB, 2026-11-12); Patrick agreed 2026-11-12 | BB, sim |
| [goblin-tackle-no-reach-3-up] | A goblin's Tackle cannot target a tile 3 heights above the goblin. | PROVEN | live watch: Patrick's own eyes, an Araguay practice battle staged by the BB simulator, hand_check_practice_1948.log in F:\FFTRec\kept\arena\three_jobs_2026-11-12 (BB, 2026-11-12); Patrick agreed 2026-11-12 | BB, sim |
| [choco-beak-reaches-1-5-up] | A chocobo's Choco Beak can target a tile 1.5 heights above the chocobo. | PROVEN | live watch: Patrick's own eyes, an Araguay practice battle staged by the BB simulator, hand_check_practice_1948.log in F:\FFTRec\kept\arena\three_jobs_2026-11-12 (BB, 2026-11-12); Patrick agreed 2026-11-12 | BB, sim |
| [venom-fang-hit-side-and-back] | A Red Panther's Venom Fang on Ramza at Brave 97 read 49 percent from his side and 82 percent from behind, so the side it comes from changes its hit. | PROVEN | live watch: Patrick's own eyes, an Araguay practice battle staged by the BB simulator, hand_check_practice_1948.log in F:\FFTRec\kept\arena\three_jobs_2026-11-12 (BB, 2026-11-12); Patrick agreed 2026-11-12 | BB, sim |
| [physical-evade-by-attack-side] | A plain Attack's hit chance falls by the target's evade according to the side it comes from: on an Archer with class evade 10 and a 40 shield the panel read 54 from her front, 60 from her side and 100 from behind, numbers that fit class and shield both counting from the front, only the shield from the side and neither from behind. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner's screen on map 80, the numbers tools/driver/hitchance.py works from her bytes by the wiki's rule, no log (BB, 2026-11-03); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |
| [hit-reads-status-and-facing-live] | The hit routine reads a unit's status bits from +0x61 and its facing bits at +0x51 live, so a write shows at the next aim. On that Archer the front number read 54 bare, 100 with Sleep set and 16 with Defending set, and with Darkness on the attacker 16 from her front, 20 once her facing turned east and 100 once it turned north. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner's screen and tools/force.py on map 80, the cursor re-aimed after each write, each number the one tools/driver/hitchance.py gives, no log (BB, 2026-11-03); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |
| [hit-reads-class-evade-from-record] | Of the record's physical evade bytes at +0x48 to +0x4B the hit routine reads only the class evade at +0x4B live: writing 25 there took the Archer's front number from 54 to 45, the rule's 100 x 60 x 75 / 10000, while 25 written to the accessory byte at +0x48 left it at 54. The PSX routine also takes class evade from the record, at 0x43. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner's screen and tools/force.py on map 80, no log (BB, 2026-11-03); PSX doc: FFHacktics wiki Equipment Evasion Setting (Physical) (BB, 2026-11-03); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |
| [hit-reads-gear-evade-from-tables] | At hit time the game takes shield evade from its item tables through the unit's hand words, not from the record's evade copies, which it sets at battle setup: a shield id written into Ramza's left hand word changed the panel's number at once, items 143, 255 and 139 reading 0, 100 and 46 at night. The PSX routine reads the accessory's and both hands' evade the same way, through the equipped ids. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner's screen and tools/force.py on map 80 at night, no log (BB, 2026-11-03); PSX doc: FFHacktics wiki Equipment Evasion Setting (Physical) (BB, 2026-11-03); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |
| [weapon-reach-from-striking-hand] | A weapon's reach is the striking hand's: the right weapon word's whenever it holds anything, else the left's, a shield being no weapon. All 32 recorded basic attacks sit within it, three archers at exactly 5. No unit holding two unequal weapons has been seen. | UNCERTAIN | PSX doc: FFHacktics wiki Weapon Range Routine, the wiki copy (BB, 2026-11-03); live tape: 32 basic attacks on the committed tapes (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [shot-weapons-skip-near-tiles] | A weapon whose attack flags neither strike nor lunge shoots, and the game's range marking never lets it aim within two tiles of the shooter, blanking those tiles after marking the diamond: guns, crossbows, bows, harps and books. The tapes hold 11 such shots, 7 with bows at 3 to 7 tiles and 4 with harps at exactly 3, none nearer. | UNCERTAIN | PSX doc: FFHacktics wiki Weapon Range Routine, the wiki copy (BB, 2026-11-03); live tape: 11 shots on the committed tapes (BB, 2026-11-03); live watch: an enemy Archer two tiles from Ramza stepped to (10,0), three tiles off, on a served plan and shot him in the back, turn 11 of the evening's driver log on map 80 (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [direct-shot-stops-at-first-body] | A direct shot stops at the first unit its line meets, and the line runs in three dimensions. A crossbow bolt aimed three tiles along a flat row at Ramza landed on an Archer beside the shooter at the shooter's height, the panel reading 0 for Ramza. With Ramza four heights up it cleared her and reached him, and with him three heights up it still landed on her. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner's screen with tools/force.py, two shots on map 80 at night, no log (BB, 2026-11-03); live watch: the owner's screen with tools/force.py, the Thief at (4,7), the Archer at (3,7) and Ramza at (1,7) three heights up, staged from a prediction before the shot, map 80, no log (BB, 2026-11-03); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |
| [weapon-flags-pick-targeting-routine] | A weapon's attack flags pick its targeting routine: an arcing weapon, the bows, takes Set and Validate Arc Trajectory, a direct one, flag 0x20 on crossbows, guns, harps and books, flies a straight line, a lunging one checks the two tiles of its lunge, and a striking one checks heights alone. | UNCERTAIN | PSX doc: FFHacktics wiki Targeting validation (weapon flags), 001b0818 and Seek Obstacle On Trajectory, the wiki copy (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [direct-shot-line-geometry] | A direct shot leaves the shooter two thirds up its sprite and aims two thirds up the target's, or at an empty tile's base, in steps of two world units, 28 to a tile side and 12 to a height. It stops inside the tile column under it or inside the first body within 8 units of a tile centre on both axes and strictly between its feet and head. A unit stands at its tile's base plus half the slope height. | [knockback-push-rules] | A critical on a single target always tries a knockback, and a formula 0x37 hit such as Throw Stone tries one when a draw under the thrower's Brave tops a draw under the target's. A blow that kills pushes nothing, in the one case seen. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: FUN_140305EB4, FUN_14030895C, FUN_140304DC0, FUN_140304F44 and FUN_140282A7C (BB, 2026-11-08); live tape: 824 recorded acts in tune_pa44.jsonl.records and sim_cast_v2_gameai.jsonl.records, 4 Throw Stone pushes against 3.2 expected and 1 critical one against 2.4, every tile matching, docs/research/sim_knockback_check_2026-11-08.txt (BB, 2026-11-08) | BB, sim |

The composed bit alone does this, though the unit still draws solid. | PROVEN | live watch: tools/probes/taunt_probe.py taunt 24 on Mandalia Plains, mod off, two control turns went for the exposed Time Mage, then three of three enemy turns passed the adjacent hidden Beowulf for the visible Time Mage, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger invisible-funnel-guardian (LW, 2026-07-22); live watch: through the shipping mod every enemy ignored the invisible units and made straight for Ramza, owner live, LW ledger invisible-funnel-guardian (LW, 2026-07-22); Patrick agreed 2026-07-22 | LW |
| [current-faith-write-scales-magic-gun] | Magic gun damage reads the caster's current Faith, +0x2D of its battle unit record (LW band +0x11), at each shot and scales linearly with it, and the damage forecast follows: Blaze Gun (id 75, formula 4) hit 45 at Faith 75, forecast and hit 55 held at 90, and hit the same target for 45 back at 75. | PROVEN | live watch: tools/probes/lw316_lane_probes.py faith_hold on Ramza against a Time Mage, 45 equalling floor(55 x 75/90), on the Denuvo build, LW ledger current-faith-write-scales-magic-gun (LW, 2026-08-25); Patrick agreed 2026-08-25 | LW |

### Maps and movement

| Id | Fact | Mark | Evidence | Leaned on by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [move-unreachable-tile-not-walked] | The game checks a Move request's destination itself: a tile the unit cannot reach is not walked to, the unit stays where it was, and the turn goes on with no crash or teleport. | PROVEN | live watch: no log, the step 0 edit of X 6 to 7 (BB, 2026-10-26); live watch: turn 2, slot 4 sent to (5,12) stayed put, brain/probe_20261026_230258.jsonl, a log lost on 2026-10-27 (BB, 2026-10-26); live tape: first_driver_battle_stub_then_opus_plays_the_enemy#6214, one of eight written moves that did not land (BB, 2026-10-27); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB, sim |
| [move-unreachable-tile-asked-again] | After refusing a Move to an on map tile the unit cannot reach, the game asks for the Move again with its own pick back in the buffer. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: no log, step 0, together's panel popped again with the stock bytes refilled (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB |
| [move-off-map-tile-no-op] | A Move request to an off map tile (X and Y 0x3F) is a no op: the unit enters move mode, does not move, and about 1.2 s later the game asks for EndTurn, with no re-ask, teleport or crash. | PROVEN | live watch: seq 254 and 255 of probe_20261026_212759.jsonl (BB, 2026-10-26); proven in BB 2026-10-26 | BB |
| [move-to-own-tile-stays] | A Move request to the unit's own tile is taken as a stay: the unit stays and the game goes on to the next phase, seven times out of seven in one battle. | PROVEN | live tape: first_driver_battle_stub_then_opus_plays_the_enemy#11630 (BB, 2026-10-27); live tape: first_driver_battle_stub_then_opus_plays_the_enemy#11632 (BB, 2026-10-27); Patrick agreed 2026-10-27 | BB, sim |
| [reach-by-walk-route-and-climb] | Whether a unit can reach a tile goes by its walking route and its climb, not grid distance: a tile 3 grid steps away but 5 walking steps round a tree and unwalkable tiles was refused six times to an Archer, and a climb of 6 was refused twice. Move 3 and Jump 3 were assumed, not read. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: first_driver_battle_stub_then_opus_plays_the_enemy#6214, #9422, #13778, #15398, #17729 and #18603 for slot 1, #10457 and #12385 for slots 2 and 8 (BB, 2026-10-27); game table: heights from the MAP080 mesh in data/enhanced/0002.pac (BB, 2026-10-27) | BB, sim |
| [map-files-in-0002-pac] | The battle maps live in data/enhanced/0002.pac under fftpack/map in the PSX format: the GNS index, its 20 byte records intact but the CD sector fields replaced by a running index, and the mesh and texture resources under their PSX numbers. | UNCERTAIN | game table: data/enhanced/0002.pac fftpack/map, read by a parser (BB, 2026-10-27) | BB, sim |
| [map-mesh-terrain-psx-layout] | Each map mesh keeps the PSX layout: a 196 byte pointer header, and the terrain at pointer 0x68 as two levels of 256 tiles of 8 bytes (surface, height, depth and slope height, slope type, thickness, walk and cursor flags). | UNCERTAIN | game table: every mesh in data/enhanced/0002.pac (BB, 2026-10-27) | BB, sim |
| [map-files-over-ganesha-json] | The game's own map files, not fft-map-json, are the truth for terrain. That Ganesha derived JSON, which FFTHandsFree ships, matches on seven fields per tile for 96 of 122 maps and differs on a few tiles or in size for the rest. | UNCERTAIN | game table: every map in data/enhanced/0002.pac against fft-map-json (BB, 2026-10-27) | BB, sim |
| [map-numbers-of-named-battles] | Araguay Woods is MAP080, Dugeura Pass MAP086, the Orbonne book storage fourth floor MAP060 and Zeklaus Desert MAP076. | UNCERTAIN | game table: every stock destination on four tapes is a walkable level 0 tile of the named map, and MAP076 alone of 122 fits the first live battle (BB, 2026-10-27); source read: FFTHandsFree's map id table, agreeing on 80, 86 and 76 (BB, 2026-10-27); live tape: s1_conversions_hand_mode_araguay_woods_ending_in_petrify, map byte 80 at Araguay Woods (BB, 2026-10-28) | BB |

| [tile-highlight-mark-byte] | The game's tile highlight mark is a per tile flag byte, not a coordinate list. One marked tile flipped bit 0x80 at three copies, 0x140e7c3bb, 0x14102fb23 and 0x14117fe67, and holding 0x80 there made the game draw the mark with no input. Released, the game clears it. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: a differential toggle scan and a hold, probes/mark_probe.py togglefind and holdmany, on a build before 1.5, no owner flip named, LW ledger treasure-master-tile-mark-writable (LW, 2026-06-11) | TM |

| [after-move-tile-event-dispatcher] | One function, 0x14020435C to 0x140204A1A, runs a tile's events after a move, picking them by bits 1, 2 and 4 of 0x142FCE878. Bit 4 is the item pickup, its crystal branches split on the ability value in esi, -1 for the restore, and its instruction at 0x1402043C8 writes the UI state 21. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: offline xref and disassembly of 0x14020435C (BB, 2026-10-29); live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-10-29.jsonl, 0x1402043C8 writing 21 (BB, 2026-10-29) | BB |
| [map080-no-walk-tiles] | Araguay Woods, MAP080, flags tiles (5,7) and (6,6) no walk at height 0 in the game's own map file, as fft-map-json does, and no unit stood on either in 19,567 unit sightings across the map 80 tapes. | [map-mesh-variants] | A map's terrain sits in a 0x2e01 mesh for 105 maps and, for 16 with no such mesh holding a chunk, in a 0x2f01 override. On 24 maps another mesh of the same map, a weather or time variant, differs in some tile byte, on 14 of them in a terrain field and on maps 105 to 114 only in bits of byte 6 above the two flags (and bit 0x80 of byte 7 on 109). Maps 60, 76, 80 and 86 have no such variant. | UNCERTAIN | game table: docs/research/map_mesh_survey_2026-11-01.json (BB, 2026-11-01); game table: tools/map_gen.py over the game's unpacked map files, docs/research/map_gen_2026-11-03.json (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [jump-over-gap-landing-not-higher] | A walking unit may jump across an unwalkable tile to the tile beyond when the landing stands no higher than the takeoff, heights taken at tile centres with the slope height halved. On Araguay Woods a unit with Move 3 and Jump 3 at (6,7) had (4,7) and (3,7) in its move range but neither (6,5), a Convex NW tile half a step above, nor (5,5). How wide the jump may be and what it costs in Move are not told apart. | PROVEN | live watch: Wilham, Move 3 and Jump 3, at (6,7) on Araguay Woods, the game's blue range matching the walk finder on all 16 tiles on the owner's screen, no log (BB, 2026-11-03); live tape: the four fully recorded tapes, 282 of 294 legal moves accepted with all eight refusals still refused, docs/research/walk_rules_2026-11-01.json (BB, 2026-11-01); live tape: the twelve held back tapes, the horizontal jump 3 of 3 on MAP080, docs/research/walk_rules_holdout_2026-11-01.json (BB, 2026-11-01); PSX doc: the PSX Spread Horizontal Jump routine inside Set Reachable Tiles, BB's research note of 2026-11-01 (BB, 2026-11-01); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |
| [height-limit-is-jump-both-ways] | A unit's height limit when it walks is its Jump, measured edge to edge, the same stepping down as climbing, so a drop has no free fall. Seven moves off the ledge at (0,6), height 8, down to height 2 by a unit with Jump 4 were refused, while drops of 6 at Jump 5 and of 4 at Jump 3 were played. This replaces two earlier fits, a free descent and a limit of Jump + 1. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: charm_invitation_stale_plan_and_a_swap_soft_lock_live#18, #2554, #3371, #3694, #5422, #7161 and #7197 with exam_two_battle_one_unwatched_auto_battle#26 and turn_end_filter_live_hand_played_pairs_and_think_ahead#1517, the refused and the played drops (BB, 2026-11-02); live tape: the Chocobo's drop of 6 at Jump 5, (9,6) to (8,2) on MAP086, docs/research/walk_rules_2026-11-01.json (BB, 2026-11-01); PSX doc: the FFHacktics movement listing, docs/research/ffhacktics_movement_rules_psx_2026-11-03.txt (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [reach-follows-psx-movement-routine] | The PC game decides where a unit may walk by the PSX reach routine. A finder written from the FFHacktics listing, not fitted, accepts all 623 moves the game played among 647 recorded moves over 4 maps and refuses all 24 it refused, with one rule added: a body whose countdown reads 0 is free ground, without which it accepts 621. | UNCERTAIN | PSX doc: the FFHacktics movement listing read in the owner's browser, docs/research/ffhacktics_movement_rules_psx_2026-11-03.txt (BB, 2026-11-03); live tape: 647 moves on the committed tapes over 4 maps (BB, 2026-11-03); live watch: the owner's look agreeing on one unit's 16 tiles, no log (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [teleport-moves-within-manhattan-move] | A teleporting unit moves by grid distance alone: it may land on any unoccupied tile within a Manhattan distance of its Move, with no path, terrain and units not in its way. A Ghoul (job 112, Move 4, Jump 4) went (7,6) to (4,6) across the unwalkable (6,6) and a 17 high tree, and all 36 teleporter moves on the tapes sat within Manhattan Move. Ghosts carry Teleport as an innate. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: the auto battle tape, three Ghouls' 28 moves all within distance 4, docs/research/walk_rules_2026-11-01.json part three (BB, 2026-11-01); live tape: 36 teleporter moves on the 17 tapes with a known map, 2 of 2 scored, docs/research/movement_abilities_2026-11-02.json (BB, 2026-11-02); PSX doc: the PSX note's teleporter rule, Manhattan distance within Move with terrain ignored (BB, 2026-11-01) | BB |
| [walk-passes-own-team-and-bodies] | A walking unit may pass through tiles held by units of its own team and by bodies. Every recorded move whose only way ran through an own team unit or a body was played: 11 of 11 by player side movers, 12 of 12 and 25 of 25 by enemy movers, and 26 of 26 crossings of allies and corpses on the held back tapes. Whether the other team blocks is not tested, no recorded move hinging on it. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: 605 moves on the 17 tapes with a known map, teams read from Flags2 bits 0x30, docs/research/blocking_by_side_2026-11-02.json (BB, 2026-11-02); live tape: the twelve held back tapes, docs/research/walk_rules_holdout_2026-11-01.json (BB, 2026-11-01) | BB |
| [charmed-unit-walked-through-old-team] | A charmed unit walked through a unit of its old team. Slot 3, Move 3, charmed and reading team 0x00 at +0x05, went (7,4) to (9,4) through slot 0, team 0x10, at (8,4), with no other route inside its Move. The walk is what was seen. Why it may pass is open. | PROVEN | live tape: charm_invitation_stale_plan_and_a_swap_soft_lock_live#3361, watched by the owner (BB, 2026-11-02); Patrick agreed 2026-11-02 | BB |
| [walk-team-from-mover-mirror-byte] | When it decides who blocks a walk the game may take the mover's own team from its copy at +0x1EE and every other unit's from +0x05, so a charmed unit walks by its original team. The PSX reach routine read that way explains the charmed walk through its old team, and that one move is all that exercises it. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: charm_invitation_stale_plan_and_a_swap_soft_lock_live#3361 scored by a finder written from the FFHacktics listing, docs/research/ffhacktics_movement_rules_psx_2026-11-03.txt (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [move-ends-on-crystal-or-treasure] | A unit may end its move on a crystal or a treasure, picking it up by stopping, and on a body whose countdown at +0x07 reads 0, but no move was seen ending on a body still counting 3, 2 or 1, and the one refused stop was onto a living ally. Of 12 moves the game played onto a dead occupant's tile, 10 ended on a crystal or a treasure and 2 on bodies at 0. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: the 13 moves onto occupied tiles with the occupant's status bytes, docs/research/corpse_split_2026-11-02.json (BB, 2026-11-02); live tape: the held back split, a no stop on a corpse rule contradicted on 11 derivation moves, docs/research/walk_rules_holdout_2026-11-01.json (BB, 2026-11-01); live tape: 647 moves on 4 maps, 623 of 623 with a zero count body as free ground and 621 without it (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [walk-stalls-without-node-destination] | A unit moved from outside without its node's walk destination at node +0x8C to +0x8E cannot finish its next walk. With y + 1 as North the PSX reach routine accepts 623 of 623 played moves and refuses 24 of 24 refused, and with y + 1 as South only 621 and 18, the six refused moves at Araguay Woods making the difference. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: 647 moves on 4 maps, docs/research/ffhacktics_movement_rules_psx_2026-11-03.txt (BB, 2026-11-03); PSX doc: FFHacktics wiki Dismount and update Facing Direction and Find Direction of Target (BB, 2026-11-03); source read: FFTLivingWeapons docs/LIVE_LEDGER.md facing-byte-band-0x35, a facing North plant rendered at y + 1 (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [tile-mark-records-and-flag] | The game draws a tile's highlight mark from a flag in per tile records, and a flag held from outside draws it with no capture step. In the list at 0x141187AD7, stride 0x20, each record holds its tile's two mesh bytes, the Y and level byte then X, 17 bytes before its flag, and lists at 0x140E8B043 and 0x14103E7AB, stride 0xB8, share its index. Holding 0xCC on a resting flag drew the mark. Two of one unit's 16 move tiles had no record. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner's screen with tools/tile_light.py lighting Wilham's tiles, no log (BB, 2026-11-03); proven in BB 2026-11-03 | BB |

Maps 74, 76, 80 and 85 drew their ground, Sweegy Woods (74) with animated textures, so animated textures are not the cause. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner's screen at keyless starts with encounter 166, maps 31, 76, 80, 85 and 91 and Orbonne's 56, no log named (BB, 2026-11-03); live watch: the owner's screen at maps 31, 32 and 74, docs/research/live_traps_2026-11-05_story_start.jsonl (BB, 2026-11-05); proven in BB 2026-11-03; proven in BB 2026-11-05 | BB |

| [mesh-loader-call-real-vs-keyless] | The map loader 0x1402516BC calls the mesh loader 0x1400C7F54 once a load, after one step 0x75 and then 0x76 until the load ends. A real start of Dorter Slums handed it state 0x1000 in r9 and 0 as the sixth argument, the battle start write state 0x8000 and 4, and a story start one scenario late matched the real one. It returned 0 for maps 31 and 85 alike. Real Orbonne loaded map 62 then 56 at state 0x2000, a keyless start 56 alone. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: traps on 0x1400C7F54 and 0x1402516BC, one real start and one battle start write of Dorter Slums, docs/research/live_traps_2026-11-05_story_start.jsonl (BB, 2026-11-05); live watch: one read each of the mesh loader for maps 31 and 85 and for Orbonne, no log named (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [map-look-from-weather-and-time] | The mesh loader's sixth argument is a look number from 0x140254B8C, which the map loader calls with the map and script variables 0x23, 0x24, 0x30 and 0x27. When 0x141853C08 is 1, a battle from the world map, the look comes from weather and time alone, 1 to 3 by day and 4 to 6 by night. Otherwise it searches the map's records for a look 0 matching time and weather. A story map with no records for looks 1 to 6 likely explains the missing ground. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: 0x140254B8C and the map loader 0x1402516BC, the map records not read (BB, 2026-11-05) | BB |
| [map-draw-object-fields] | The drawing side's map object sits at [[0x143CC96B8]+0x24270]: +0x4800C holds the loaded map or -1, +0x48014 the state and +0x48000 the event being played. The mesh loader runs only when +0x4800C is -1, and otherwise 0x1400D55C4 runs. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: the mesh loader 0x1400C7F54 and its map object (BB, 2026-11-05) | BB |
| [map-load-reads-whole-index] | A map load reads every file of the map's index, all states, back to back into one block in index order, each rounded up to 0x800 bytes, the old primary mesh among them, with the new one from new_map in a second block. A load that drew no ground held the same files, so the files in memory do not tell a good load from a bad one. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: a memory scan for each map file's bytes, Sweegy Woods, Dorter and Dorter Slums, no log named (BB, 2026-11-05) | BB |

| [araguay-encounter-97-layout] | Araguay Woods' first encounter, number 97, gives each enemy kind its own slot and start tile, each present or absent per start: slot 0 a black goblin on (5,1), slots 1 to 5 goblins on (7,0), (4,0), (3,1), (10,1) and (9,0), slot 6 job 118 on (7,0), slots 7, 9 and 10 job 112 on (4,0), (10,1) and (9,0), and slot 8 job 104 on (3,1), or elsewhere when a goblin holds that tile. | [picture-node-two-tile-copies] | A unit's picture node keeps its tile twice, at +0x88 and +0x8C, and a walk starts from the second. With only the first written, a goblin moved from (3,1) to (7,0) planned its walk from (3,1) and its picture shot across the map. With both written, a goblin moved to (4,0) walked to (4,2) and a ghoul moved to (10,1) walked to (9,4), each out of reach of its dealt tile. | [walking-height-half-steps] | On the PSX a tile's walking height in half steps is 2 x tile height plus the low 5 bits of tile byte +3. Depth, the top 3 bits, adds 2 x depth only for a unit that floats, walks on water or moves in water, with 2 more or 2 less for the first and last, though the page's comment says a plain walker adds it. A tile's number is x + y x width, plus 256 on the upper layer. | UNCERTAIN | PSX doc: FFHacktics wiki Calculate Height Data and Map Location Calculation (BB, 2026-11-06) | BB |
| [no-tile-height-table-found] | No tile height table sits where the PSX keeps one: the 0x1000 bytes before the battle unit table hold only 14 non zero bytes, and TerrainGrid at 0x140C6B440 is not one either, its 217 records naming 41 Araguay tiles with no byte matching height. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: where.py style reads, pid 4796, a Mandalia battle and the world map (BB, 2026-11-06) | BB |

A fan guide names four wet battlefields, a gap not looked into. | UNCERTAIN | PSX doc: the owner's Fandom table with the FFHacktics wiki Load Map Data (BB, 2026-11-05); source read: map 74 read as a swamp of 28 Swamp tiles, and the MAP080.json header (BB, 2026-11-05); source read: a fan guide the owner pasted (BB, 2026-11-06) | BB |
| [zeklaus-degenerator-trap-0-6] | Zeklaus Desert, map 76, has a level reducing trap, a degenerator, on tile (0,6): a unit that comes to rest there loses one level at once. Levels fell there 11 times on the recordings and nowhere else, never in 20 stops on (6,9), whose degenerator the trap table marks off. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: probe_20261110_145535#76505 with its video and three more in docs/research/zeklaus_second_model_plan_2026-11-09.txt, four stops and four falls in 30 battles (BB, 2026-11-10); live tape: 60 recorded battles, trap_stops_second_fight.out in pin_fix_2026-11-11 (BB, 2026-11-11) | BB |
| [trap-tile-table-in-exe] | The exe holds a table of hidden item and trap tiles, 6 bytes a tile and four tiles a map in map order. Byte 0 is the tile, x in the high half and y in the low, and byte 1 the flags: 0x10 off, 0x08 steel needle, 0x04 sleeping gas, 0x02 death trap, 0x01 degenerator. Map 76 reads (0,6) degenerator, (2,10) steel needle, (6,2) sleeping gas and (6,9) degenerator marked off, and map 80 all four off. | UNCERTAIN | exe scan: trap_table.py in second_fight_2026-11-09, found once by the signature the FFHacktics page on this build's data tables gives (BB, 2026-11-09) | BB |
| [unit-teleport-three-layer-write] | A unit's position lives in three layers, its record's tile ([unit-grid-xy-bytes]), its render node's AI tile key and the node's world transform. Writing all three together moves it completely, so it stands, paths and acts from the new tile, and two units can swap. The game re-adopts every layer after the unit's next real move and re-stamps them at turn open. | PROVEN | live watch: a ghost Knight slid tile by tile on world X pokes and a full Ramza and enemy swap on all layers left both acting normally, swap_units.py, owner live, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger teleport-swap-float-render-node-transform (LW, 2026-07-10); live watch: the owner's screen and tools/force.py on map 80, the Archer written to (3,2) stood there and the next turn file listed her there, at (5,1) a crossbow bolt found her, and Ramza written to (7,1) was targetable with his own evade numbers, no log (BB, 2026-11-03); Patrick agreed 2026-07-21; proven in BB 2026-11-03 | LW, BB |
| [unit-on-unpathable-tile-stranded] | The game accepts a unit placed on a tile it would never path to, such as a treetop: it draws perched with its turn marker and health bar, the Height readout re-derives (17), and it acts normally, ranged attacks included, but cannot move off (At present, can't move to any other tiles), and enemy AI backs off without engaging. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner warped a unit onto a treetop, a stone it threw hit an enemy, and nothing crashed or froze, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger teleport-swap-float-render-node-transform (LW, 2026-07-22); proven in LW 2026-07-22 | LW |
| [render-z-cosmetic-and-hit-restamp] | A unit's drawn height does not matter for targeting, since the AI reads the logic tile: a unit floating above its new tile was attacked normally, and being hit snapped it to the ground, so a hit re-stamps the height as a move's end and turn open do. | PROVEN | live watch: a unit warped sideways kept the old tile's height and floated, an enemy attacked it with no difficulty and the hit snapped it to ground level, owner live, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger teleport-swap-float-render-node-transform (LW, 2026-07-22); proven in LW 2026-07-22 | LW |
| [world-z-downward-floor-clamped] | World Z grows downward, -12 per height level, and a downward write does not sink a unit into the terrain: it stops at the floor, while an upward write is free. | PROVEN | live watch: owner live, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger teleport-swap-float-render-node-transform (LW, 2026-07-22); proven in LW 2026-07-22 | LW |
| [co-tiled-units-soft-lock] | Two units on one tile shadow each other for targeting in slot order, and moving one soft locks at the movement confirm. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: seen when a hidden unit was restored onto an occupied tile, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger hide-reveal-ghost-statue (LW, 2026-07-10); live watch: recorded as the teleport guard, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger teleport-swap-float-render-node-transform (LW, 2026-07-10) | LW |
| [terrain-grid-cursor-source] | The terrain grid at 0x140D8DCC0, 8 bytes a tile at index x + y times map width + level bit times 0x100, holds real per tile height in the low 5 bits of byte +2. The game reads it live for the inspected tile's height panel and camera pan, and its reader 0x14027B25A takes the height of the tile a unit stands on. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: a whole map dump drew a coherent 11 by 12 heightmap matching the battlefield and a 10 by 18 map did too, and a held height moved the hover camera pan, owner witnessed, tools/probes/terrain_probe.py, on the Denuvo build, filed under Proven but SELF_TEST_PLAN Appendix A finds its pathfinding half contradicted, LW ledger terrain-grid-cursor-source (LW, 2026-07-27); live watch: a Cheat Engine what accesses capture on a bystander's +0x4F caught 0x14027B25A computing the index with imul ecx,r14d, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger terrain-grid-cursor-source (LW, 2026-07-27) | LW |
| [terrain-grid-is-pathfinding-input] | The terrain grid at 0x140D8DCC0 is the pathfinder's live input, so writing a tile's height or flags changes where units can move. | CONTRADICTED | live watch: a 4 tile ring held on all nine in memory copies changed only the one inspected tile, left move ranges and enemy paths alone, and a goblin walked through it, the earlier tile not lit being a tile already out of range, and f3 = 20 for 90 s and f0 bits 0xC0 for 60 s blocked nothing, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger terrain-grid-cursor-source (LW, 2026-07-28) | LW |
| [terrain-grid-writes-last-session] | Writes to the terrain grid last for the whole game process, through battle restarts and onto the world map, and only a relaunch clears them. Bit 0 of byte +6 left set crashed the game. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: owner, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger terrain-grid-cursor-source (LW, 2026-07-28) | LW |
| [map-dims-byte-pair] | The u8 pair at 0x140C6AD6A and 0x140C6AD6B holds the battle map's width and height: it read 11 by 12 and then 10 by 18 on two maps, each matching, and the pathfinder's width register held 11 on the 11 wide map. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: two live maps and a Cheat Engine capture, a two map correlation not yet an isolated proof, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger terrain-grid-cursor-source (LW, 2026-07-27) | LW |
| [attack-reach-map-classifier] | The routine 0x14027FE20 gives a targetability verdict over the reach map at 0x1418004E0, marking the tiles a unit's swing can reach: it marked the four neighbouring tiles for a sword's swing. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: instrument run with the owner at the controls, the Ravager's own swing, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger empty-tile-hand-read-bounds (LW, 2026-08-31) | LW |

### Abilities and jobs

| Id | Fact | Mark | Evidence | Leaned on by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [action-table-filled-at-boot] | The ability action table at 0x14078B2DC (368 rows of 20 bytes) is a blank template in the exe file, every row FF FF FF FF then sixteen zeros, and the game fills it during boot. Once a save has loaded, row 189 holds the bytes the filled copy at 0x14078961C carries in the file (byte +15 = 0x2D), and the FF FF FF FF row markers survive the fill. | PROVEN | exe scan: tools/probes/static_table_file_image_probe.py against stamp 0x6A5E4A1B, decoy row 189 FF FF FF FF 05 00 00 01 11 12 00 00 38 00 00 2D 00 00 00 00, LW ledger action-table-live-copy-filled-at-boot (LW, 2026-10-17); live watch: the two LW-399 legs, provoke read ON after Continue and a Defender wielder got its grant with no capture refusal, LW ledger action-table-live-copy-filled-at-boot (LW, 2026-10-17); live watch: row 189 read live as range 5, formula 56, MP 0, CT 0, inflict 0x2D, LW ledger cut-ability-provoke-recipe (LW, 2026-07-22); Patrick agreed 2026-10-17 | LW, BB |
| [zeroed-reaction-bits-stop-reactions] | A unit's reaction abilities are a 4 byte field at +0x94 of its battle unit record. Holding it at zero stops the unit reacting, and restoring the bytes brings the reaction back. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: Counter stayed silent through 5 hits, probes/cripple_probe.py, on a build before 1.5, no owner flip named, LW ledger reaction-suppression (LW, 2026-06-09) | LW |
| [support-bits-grantable] | A unit's support abilities are a 4 byte bitfield at +0x98 of its battle unit record. A bit OR-set mid battle works for supports the damage calculation checks, Concentration among them, while HP Boost, MP Boost, Doublehand and Dual Wield are applied when the unit is built and do nothing when set later. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: Concentration and others in play, the four build time supports read back fine with no effect, on a build before 1.5, no owner flip named, LW ledger support-bits-grantable (LW, 2026-06-08) | LW |
| [movement-exactly-one] | Only one movement ability is in effect at a time: with two font movement bits held perfectly, the game honoured only Lifefont. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: the retired Spiritual Font bit grant attempt, on a build before 1.5, no owner flip named, LW ledger movement-exactly-one (LW, 2026-06-10) | LW |
| [master-teleportation-grant] | Holding a unit's movement ability bits at the image for Master Teleportation (id 243) mid battle lets a player unit teleport. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: a player unit teleported in session, on a build before 1.5, no owner flip named, LW ledger master-teleportation-grant (LW, 2026-06-10) | LW |
| [jobcommand-slot-grants-ability] | Writing an ability id into a slot of a job's command record, with that slot's extend bit set and the unit's learned bit for it held, makes the ability usable from that command, as a primary or a secondary. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: Barrage cast end to end from a Thief primary and from Steal as a secondary, on a build before 1.5, no owner flip named, LW ledger jobcommand-inject-grant (LW, 2026-06-10) | LW |
| [reach-of-aim-throw-and-items] | Throw, the item abilities and Aim take their reach from the game's range switch, not an action table row. Attack and Aim share one case and take the weapon's reach. Throw reaches the thrower's own Move, the 0x80 flag on its range byte clearing the thrower's own tile, so a throw may land beside the thrower but never on it. An item reaches one tile, or four with the Throw Items support or on a Mime. Jump has its own routine. | UNCERTAIN | PSX doc: FFHacktics wiki Calculate Target Panels, Glain's listing, and Set Target Tiles, the wiki copy (BB, 2026-11-03); live tape: an Aim that landed at 7 with a bow and a Ninja who threw at exactly 4, its Move, on the committed tapes (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [file-action-table-row-layout] | The ability action table the exe file carries filled, at 0x14078961C, is 368 rows of 20 bytes, all with the 0xFF marker but row 0, the basic Attack. A row holds range at +4, effect area +5, vertical +6, four flag bytes +7 to +10, element +11, formula +12, X +13, Y +14, status +15, CT +16 and MP +17. | UNCERTAIN | exe scan: tools/ability_table.py over the exe's file image, PE stamp 0x6A5E4A1B, written to tools/exam/abilities.json (BB, 2026-11-03); live tape: all 270 recorded Acts name a skillset and ability the table lists, the basic Attack aside, on the 18 committed tapes (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [action-range-zero-flags] | In the action table 60 rows have range 0: 13 carry the weapon range flag, bit 0x20 of flag byte 1 (Attack, the Rend family, Leg Shot, Arm Shot, Seal Evil and row 358), and 46 aim at their caster, bit 0x02. | UNCERTAIN | exe scan: tools/ability_table.py over the exe's file image, PE stamp 0x6A5E4A1B (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |
| [human-skillset-records] | Human skillsets are 25 byte records at 0x14067E210: two flag bytes, whose bits each add 0x100 to one slot's id, over 16 low id bytes, most significant bit first. 111 of the 176 hold something. | UNCERTAIN | exe scan: tools/ability_table.py over the exe's file image, PE stamp 0x6A5E4A1B (BB, 2026-11-03) | BB |

Tape first sights fit, equal or a tick late. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: Ghidra 12.1.4 headless on a copy of the exe, sha256 937233f7fe76182a, docs/research/ghidra_trial_plan_2026-11-07.txt (BB, 2026-11-07); live tape: the tapes' records, Aim +2 first seen at 4, 4 and 3 and Aim +4 at 5 and 5 (BB, 2026-11-06) | BB |

No unit was seen with and without it on one spell. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: ctape.py over the tapes' records (BB, 2026-11-06) | BB |

The 2 uses cannot tell adding 1 from setting 18. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: probe_20261108_190353#29767 to #29770 and probe_20261104_135309#389185, the only 2 recorded uses (BB, 2026-11-08); PSX doc: the FFHacktics page for this formula, adding Y (BB, 2026-11-08); Ghidra read: FUN_14030A488, the rebuild, the formula routine not read on this build (BB, 2026-11-08) | BB |

| [jobcommand-record-table] | Job command records sit at 0x14067E213 + record times 25: sixteen ability slots after a flag prefix 3 bytes before, extend bits read most significant first, and each unit's learned bits at roster +0x32 + job index times 3. The game reads a record live, so rewriting all sixteen slots swaps the whole command for every unit of that job, enemies included. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: record 14's Steal slots rewritten to the Knight's Rend 138 to 145 and then to a Thief, Knight and Monk mix both listed and cast, and a puppeted enemy Wizard's Black Magic record listed Rend Helm and Rend MP, Patrick cast them, on a build before 1.5.2, no owner flip named, SELF_TEST_PLAN Appendix A, LW ledger full-kit-transplant (LW, 2026-06-18); live watch: an enemy Knight carried the job global Provoke grant as a learned command, on a build likely before 1.5.2, LW ledger zero-value-command-ai-silent (LW, 2026-07-23) | LW |

| [action-table-first-copy-inert] | The ability action table has two copies one table length apart, and the game and its UI consult only the second, 0x14078B2DC: an edit to the first, 0x14078961C, reads back but changes nothing, while the same edit to the second changed the ability card and the cast at once. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: byte +15 held at 0 in the first copy left Immobilize landing at 100 percent, and the same edit in the second removed it from the card and the cast, owner live, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger cut-ability-provoke-recipe (LW, 2026-07-22) | LW |
| [action-table-row-layout] | An ability action row is 20 bytes laid out as the modloader's OverrideAbilityActionData schema: +0 to +3 unused (FF FF FF FF), +4 range (0 lets the game supply it), +5 effect area, +6 vertical, +7 to +10 Flags1 to Flags4, +11 element, +12 formula, +13 X, +14 Y, +15 inflict status, +16 CT, +17 MP cost, +18 and +19 padding, zero on all 736 entries. | UNCERTAIN | game table: both copies read live, 736 physical entries, tools/probes/ability_table_probe.py, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger cut-ability-provoke-recipe (LW, 2026-07-22); source read: the modloader's OverrideAbilityActionData nex schema, LW ledger cut-ability-provoke-recipe (LW, 2026-07-22) | LW |
| [action-row-element-bits] | Byte +11 of an ability action row is an element bitfield: 0x80 Fire, 0x40 Lightning, 0x20 Ice, 0x10 Wind, 0x08 Earth, 0x04 Water, 0x02 Holy, 0x01 Dark. 74 rows set it, each a single bit except Hallowed Bolt (158), Holy and Lightning. | UNCERTAIN | game table: the live action table read by tools/probes/ability_table_probe.py, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger cut-ability-provoke-recipe (LW, 2026-07-22) | LW |

There is one copy, a row written into an unused index applies its status like a shipped one, and a row whose mode is 0x00 applies nothing. | PROVEN | live watch: a hand written row at index 29 with mode 0x80 first applied its status at 100 percent, after the same row read one byte late at 0x14080FBA1 kept mode 0x00 and cast at 0 percent, and findrow saw row 55's pattern once in 256 MB, tools/probes/ability_table_probe.py, owner live, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger cut-ability-provoke-recipe (LW, 2026-07-22); live watch: authored row 29 landed the mark at 100 percent in the regression pass, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger cut-ability-provoke-recipe (LW, 2026-08-12); Patrick agreed 2026-08-12 | LW |

| [ability-common-table-ai-flag] | The ability common data table at 0x140787F80 is 512 rows of 8 bytes in one copy: JP cost, learn chance and type, then four AI bytes the AI reads at 0x140787F84 + id x 8. UsableByAI is bit 0x80 of byte +7, set on 401 rows. Ability 189's row reads 00 00 64 41 12 00 00 80, and Rush's AI bytes match Attack's. | UNCERTAIN | game table: tools/probes/ability_grant_probe.py aiflag 189, its byte at 0x14078856F, no twin copy, on a build likely before 1.5.2, LW ledger zero-value-command-ai-silent (LW, 2026-07-23); Ghidra read: FUN_140320504, FUN_1403099B0 and FUN_14030A410 (BB, 2026-11-08) | LW, BB |
| [job-status-immunity-spread] | Status immunity is common among jobs: of 173 jobs, 25 resist Berserk, 22 Slow, 16 Immobilize and 3 the system statuses, and Netherseer (job 18), job 28 and Cleric (job 44) are immune to 37 of the 38 listed statuses, taking only KO. | UNCERTAIN | game table: ImmuneStatus across all 173 jobs in JobData.xml, LW ledger invented-status-id0-mark (LW, 2026-07-22) | LW |
| [monster-jobs-carry-poach-keys] | Each monster job row in the game's Job sheet carries its common and rare PoachItem keys in two unlabelled columns, 0 and 0 on human jobs. Monster jobs start at 94 Chocobo and 95 Black Chocobo, and the PoachItem sheet has 96 rows, 48 species each with a common and a rare carcass. | PROVEN | game table: nxd/poachitem.en.nxd decoded with FF16Tools and Nenkai's nex layouts and joined to the Job sheet, hand checked on six species, LW ledger poachers-den-carcass-store (LW, 2026-08-12); live watch: a Black Chocobo read job 95 in the owner's live pass, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger poachers-den-carcass-store (LW, 2026-08-12); Patrick agreed 2026-08-12 | LW |
| [job-94-is-dark-knight] | Job 94 in a battle unit record is the Dark Knight. | CONTRADICTED | live watch: an enemy read job 94 in a random battle, taken then as Dark Knight, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger victim-identity-sane-at-credit (LW, 2026-07-05); game table: the Job sheet puts the Chocobo at 94 and a Black Chocobo read 95 live, LW ledger poachers-den-carcass-store (LW, 2026-08-12) | LW |

### Items, gear and inventory

| Id | Fact | Mark | Evidence | Leaned on by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [item-stats-table-filled-at-boot] | The item stats table at 0x14080F690 (128 rows of 8 bytes) holds vanilla values in the exe file, and the game fills it at boot or save load from the item data the modloader may override, so a mod's rebalanced WP sits there once a save has loaded. Its addressing instruction is at 0x1402B8CBC. | PROVEN | exe scan: tools/probes/static_table_file_image_probe.py against stamp 0x6A5E4A1B, of 121 weapon ids 72 match the baseline only, 42 both and none the proposed value only, LW ledger item-stats-table-filled-at-boot (LW, 2026-10-17); game table: data/items.json baseline and proposed WP, LW ledger item-stats-table-filled-at-boot (LW, 2026-10-17); live watch: the two LW-399 legs, the item stats check armed and the part read ON after Continue, LW ledger item-stats-table-filled-at-boot (LW, 2026-10-17); Patrick agreed 2026-10-17 | LW, BB |
| [weapon-stats-read-through-accessor-pointer] | Every consumer of a weapon's stats, the equip card and battle damage among them, reads its 8 byte ITEM_WEAPON_DATA row only through the pointer the weapon stat accessor returns, so the row need not sit inside the game's 128 row table. | PROVEN | live watch: the accessor answered a pointer into a mod page, the card read that row, the swing did weapon damage and the kill credited, row 01 8E 01 FF 0F 00 00 00 read back, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger weapon-stat-row-stub (LW, 2026-08-27); live watch: a donor swap 67 to 37 made the card read 28, journal STAT FUNNEL, LW ledger weapon-stat-row-stub (LW, 2026-08-26); proven in LW 2026-08-27 | LW |
| [per-item-accessor-entries] | The game answers per item questions through small accessor routines keyed by item id, among them range index 0x1402B8BCC, range base 0x1402B8C0C, sprite and palette pair 0x1402B8E60, four siblings 0x1402B8CD4, 0x1402B8D3C, 0x1402B8DA0 and 0x1402B8E04, and validity 0x1402B8EBC. The accessor entries were E9 jump thunks on the Denuvo build and sit at the same RVAs on the Denuvo free build. | PROVEN | live watch: all redirected to id 37 for ids 261 to 511, id 261 took the Chaos Blade's swing art and damage, LW ledger capbreak-swing-art-via-accessor-clones (LW, 2026-08-27); live watch: per id donor tables behind each entry, all nine read back live as donor 37, LW ledger donor-table-thunk-stub (LW, 2026-08-27); live watch: ten accessor redirects armed on the Denuvo free build and Warbrand swung for weapon damage, LW ledger denuvo-free-build-relocates-under-aslr (LW, 2026-10-16); Patrick agreed 2026-08-27; Patrick agreed 2026-10-16 | LW, HF |
| [reserved-id-equality-list-gate] | The can equip routine 0x1402886D0 refuses item ids 262, 288, 293, 301 and 310 by an equality chain over five constants, not a bound (the 262 compare sits at 0x140288709), and a twin at 0x140396F52 runs for jobs 161 and 164 only. The 0x106 immediates elsewhere in the exe are zlib MIN_LOOKAHEAD constants, not item caps. | PROVEN | live watch: whatever item sat at id 262 was refused by every job while 261 and 263 equipped, and with the five constants moved high the Terrastaff at 262 equipped on an Oracle and a Geomancer, re-test 7 on the Denuvo build, LW ledger reserved-id-equality-list-gate (LW, 2026-08-30); Ghidra read: the equality chain in 0x1402886D0 and its twin, LW351_plan.md round 6, LW ledger reserved-id-equality-list-gate (LW, 2026-08-30); Patrick agreed 2026-08-31 | LW |
| [order-template-housekeeper-family] | Three copies of the game's owned item chart maintainer keep each menu order chart equal to the owned set: 0x140285F80 (inventory), 0x140286070 (picker) and 0x14039684C (owned from 0x143C52740, likely the shop commit). Each walk stops at the 0x00FF marker or a word at or above 0x105, a bound made by lea r14d,[r15+6] (r15d 0xFF) at 0x140285FB5, 0x1402860AE and 0x140396881, a disp8 imm32 scans miss. | PROVEN | Ghidra read: LW351_plan.md rounds 8 and 8b and the round 8b verify, LW ledger order-template-housekeeper-family (LW, 2026-08-31); live watch: re-test 9 with the three bounds widened, Sort clean, every owned design listed, markers intact, tools/probes/lw351_template_watch.py, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger order-template-housekeeper-family (LW, 2026-08-31); Patrick agreed 2026-08-31 | LW |
| [order-chart-walk-hazards] | Since a chart walk stops only at the 0x00FF marker or a word at or above its bound, an id past the bound inside a chart ends it early: each insert then shifts only the prefix, doubling one neighbour and destroying one word, until the marker is lost. A chart with no marker walks off the end of its memory and crashes the game. | PROVEN | live watch: the owner's screen showed five doubled shields, five vanished designs, an empty row and a crash, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger order-template-housekeeper-family (LW, 2026-08-31); live watch: a marker less template copied onto a page followed by zeros crashed the game on a Ravager purchase at 06:31, LW ledger order-templates-relocatable (LW, 2026-08-31); Patrick agreed 2026-08-31 | LW |
| [order-templates-relocatable] | Every live reader of the two weapon menu order templates (inventory 0x1407B2550, picker 0x141874540) goes through three pointer table slots, 0x14067F498[0], 0x140689C38[0] and 0x14067FA90[0], and every direct lea naming a template is a whole save struct copy. Re-pointing the three slots moves the working templates, and the game inserts, deletes, lists, sorts and saves through the new home. | PROVEN | live watch: both templates moved to a page at 0x13FF00000, its markers stepped 141 to 143 on two purchases and down to 133 on ten sales, the old blocks unchanged by a byte, a save and reload kept 143, tools/probes/lw371_order_template_relocate.py, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger order-templates-relocatable (LW, 2026-08-31); Ghidra read: a read only sweep of the running game found 15 plain code leas, all bulk copies, and the 3 pointer slots, LW ledger order-templates-relocatable (LW, 2026-08-31); Patrick agreed 2026-08-31 | LW |
| [order-template-141-word-ceiling] | Each weapon order template's save field is 141 words, so the game holds at most 140 kinds plus the 0x00FF marker. At 141 owned kinds the housekeeper pushes the marker past word 140 on both templates, the picker's overflow lands on the first word of the helmet template, which then rebuilds in insert order, and saves written then carry the marker less field. | PROVEN | live watch: with 141 kinds owned the rebuild read no 00FF end marker in its first 141 words at 06:30:37 and again at 06:40:20, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger order-templates-relocatable (LW, 2026-08-31); live watch: the autosave at 06:30:48 and the saves written 06:41:56 to 06:43:28 carry the marker less 141 id field, LW ledger order-templates-relocatable (LW, 2026-08-31); Patrick agreed 2026-08-31 | LW |
| [menu-rebuild-needs-unique-sort-keys] | The menu order rebuild places each weapon row by its item.en SortOrder key, the stock DLC rows 256 to 259 included, so two weapon rows sharing a key break both order charts (one survivor, one stale hole) and the new id drops out of the equip grid. | PROVEN | live watch: the stage 1 failure, the Moonblade's baked key 216 collided with the stock Akademy Blade (id 257, key 216), word 127 a survivor and word 128 a stale hole, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger menu-rebuild-needs-unique-sort-keys (LW, 2026-08-30); live watch: re-test 3 with every key unique rebuilt hole free, LW ledger menu-rebuild-needs-unique-sort-keys (LW, 2026-08-30); Patrick agreed 2026-08-31 | LW |
| [inventory-reset-zeroes-bag-counts] | The per item state initializer 0x140284500 zeroes the bag count of every id up to its bound (the store at 0x140284561) and refills only ids 0 to 260 from the save struct. Nothing on disk calls it directly, and it ran at a load edge and once after a battle. | PROVEN | live watch: after one battle every extended bag count read 0 while vanilla counts survived, livingweapon.log 2026-08-30 23:24 to 23:29, tools/probes/lw351_bag_watch.py, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger inventory-reset-hook-keeps-extended-counts (LW, 2026-08-30); live watch: re-test 9, a detour's canary fired at the load edge, LW ledger inventory-reset-hook-keeps-extended-counts (LW, 2026-08-31); Patrick agreed 2026-08-31 | LW |
| [item-count-lists-relocatable] | The bag count list 0x1411A7C00 and its sibling flag list 0x1411A7700 are reached through 45 plain code fields on the 1.5.2 Denuvo build, 24 rip relative and 21 image relative of the [idx + r8 + rva] shape with r8 the image base. ### Party roster

| Id | Fact | Mark | Evidence | Leaned on by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [mid-battle-level-drift] | A level up in battle updates the battle unit records at once, while the roster keeps the pre battle level until the battle ends. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: the Phoenix Down kill credit chain that a roster level match fixed, on a build before 1.5, no owner flip named, LW ledger mid-battle-level-drift (LW, 2026-06-10) | LW |

| [roster-base-and-stride] | The party roster sits at 0x1411A7D10, rows of 0x258 bytes, Ramza's row first with nameId 1 at +0x230. It holds no rows until a save loads. | PROVEN | live watch: tools/probes/nameid_unique_probe.py roster read 42 occupied rows from the owner's save, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger party-nameid-unique-key (LW, 2026-08-17); live watch: the shipped scout read not found before the save and found the base at its pin within one rescan of the load, owner drill, smoke row 6.5, on 1.5.1, LW ledger anchorscan-tier2-roster-structural-confirm (LW, 2026-07-14); Patrick agreed 2026-08-17 | LW |
| [party-nameid-unique-key] | Every occupied party roster row has a nonzero nameId, a u16 at +0x230, that no other row shares (42 rows on the one save sampled), and player nameIds stay the same across battle loads. ### Menus, screens and text

| Id | Fact | Mark | Evidence | Leaned on by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [cleared-human-bit-shows-guest] | With the human control bit cleared on the party at battle entry, the game plays the party by computer and labels its units Guest. | PROVEN | live watch: Unit Control's Control Player Units left off, brain/probe_20261027_011757.jsonl, a log no longer kept (BB, 2026-10-27); proven in BB 2026-10-27 | BB |
| [menu-list-builder-caps] | The shared menu list builder 0x140288B94 caps a list with the byte at 0x140288CC3 (vanilla 0x91, 145 entries) and an insert bound imm32 at 0x14028631A (vanilla 0x92). The Items tab hands it the static 256 word buffer 0x141811470, while the equip picker and the shops hand it stack buffers that hold 152 words before the stack cookie. | PROVEN | live watch: a detour at the builder's 16 byte prologue sent the stack callers to a mod buffer and the caps went to 255 with every menu safe, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger list-builder-buffer-swap (LW, 2026-08-31); live watch: the caps read 0x91 and 0x92 before a probe patched them, LW ledger order-templates-relocatable (LW, 2026-08-31); Ghidra read: the plan review's frame arithmetic on disk, LW ledger order-templates-relocatable (LW, 2026-08-31); Patrick agreed 2026-08-31 | LW |
| [menu-lists-draw-past-149-rows] | Menu screens draw lists far past the vanilla cap once the builder's caps are raised and its stack buffers kept safe: the Items tab drew 235 weapon rows, a Dorter shop 161 rows and the All Items view 153, with clean scrolling and Sort. | PROVEN | live watch: tools/probes/lw372_list_cap_probe.py raised the caps to 255 and 256, the housekeeper grew the chart to 251 kinds and the builder emitted 235 entries into 0x141811470, the owner scrolled and sorted, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger items-tab-draws-past-149 (LW, 2026-08-31); live watch: the 161 row shop and 153 row All Items lists counted by the owner and matched word for word by a read of the list buffer, LW ledger list-builder-buffer-swap (LW, 2026-08-31); Patrick agreed 2026-08-31 | LW |
| [picker-appends-two-hand-items] | The equip picker's weapons only caller 0x1402875A4 appends the unit's two hand items after the capped list and before its terminator, so its buffer needs the cap plus 3 words, and 149 is the largest cap its 152 word stack buffer survives. | PROVEN | Ghidra read: the second verification of LW-371 and the frame arithmetic on disk, re-derived 11:05, LW ledger order-templates-relocatable (LW, 2026-08-31); live watch: the equip picker opened with both hands full on two units at 149 entries, no crash, LW ledger list-builder-buffer-swap (LW, 2026-08-31); Patrick agreed 2026-08-31 | LW |
| [party-browse-byte-zero-or-one] | The party browse byte 0x140D408E2 reads only 0 or 1 on a loaded save. | PROVEN | live watch: the two LW-400 legs on the dev build, the twin lane armed on Continue with no warning naming a flag value, LW ledger twin-menu-flags-zero-at-map-time (LW, 2026-10-17); live tape: lw193_menusig_20260817_110948.log, one save and one session, LW ledger twin-menu-flags-zero-at-map-time (LW, 2026-08-17); Patrick agreed 2026-10-17 | LW |

| [inline-color-markup-in-ui-text] | UI text carries inline colour markup that the renderer reads at draw time: a well formed `<color=NN>` tag recolours the text after it up to a bare `<color>` or `</color>`, and a malformed or orphaned tag shows as literal characters. The weapon description card uses the same parser, where color 80 reads yellow. | PROVEN | live watch: two edits of the world map Camera Controls help text, the broken tag showing as literal characters and the well formed one recolouring, LW ledger inline-color-markup-in-ui-text (LW, 2026-08-24); live watch: tools/probes/lw307_card_markup_probe.py rewrote Warbrand's flavor line with a color 80 tag and the span drew bright yellow with no literal tag characters, owner screenshot, LW ledger inline-color-markup-in-ui-text (LW, 2026-08-25); proven in LW 2026-08-25 | LW |
| [ui-text-named-colors-and-icons] | The help panel's text markup also takes named colour tokens such as `<color=abilityhint_cap_01>` and inline icons such as `<icon=104>`, closed with `</color>`. | UNCERTAIN | source read: the Knight Overhaul mod fftivc.jepoy.jobs.knight 1.0.1, its uijobabilityhelp.en.nxd strings, LW ledger inline-color-markup-in-ui-text (LW, 2026-08-24) | LW |
| [menu-icon-two-cache-layers] | The game keeps every drawn menu icon in two caches, a UITextureParts entry per UI context and one texture record per .tex, and loads it from modded.pac again only when a screen open finds neither. With only the parts entry gone the icon vanishes at once, then reloads stale from the texture record. | PROVEN | live watch: both layers hidden by name, the Claymore drew the red marker art and then its own tier 2 glow with no restart, tools/probes/lw381_tex_cache_rename.py and lw381_parts_cache_rename.py, LW ledger icon-cache-hide-reload (LW, 2026-09-01); live watch: the same pac patch with no hide left both surfaces unchanged through section switches, a list reopen and a save load, LW ledger icon-cache-hide-reload (LW, 2026-09-01); Patrick agreed 2026-09-01 | LW |
| [ui-texture-parts-entry-layout] | A UITextureParts entry is 0x70 bytes: a name pointer at +0 to its UITextureParts:// URI, an interned id at +8, texture handles at +0x50 and a state u16 at +0x58 that reads 0x0101 once loaded, with no pointer to its texture record. A texture record holds its asset path pointer at +0, the .tex size at +0x28 and the low 32 bits of the pac path hash at +0x30. | PROVEN | live watch: entries found by tools/probes/lw381_parts_cache_probe.py and hidden by name pointer +1 with id and hash zeroed, LW ledger icon-cache-hide-reload (LW, 2026-09-01); Patrick agreed 2026-09-01 | LW |
| [icon-cache-lookup-routine] | The icon cache lookup 0x1403FBB80 is entry* Lookup(manager rcx, key rdx), with key+0 the URI and key+8 the interned id. It matches on the id when both sides carry one and otherwise by strcmp of the name, over the slot array at manager+0xE0 to +0xE8. | PROVEN | Ghidra read: prologues read from the exe with identity 0x6A5E4A1B and 0x03F9E000, the call site census re derived by a second reviewer, LW ledger icon-cache-heal-on-miss (LW, 2026-10-18); live watch: a hook there forwarded every call and healed misses within milliseconds, dev build of e8c5fbe6, LW ledger icon-cache-heal-on-miss (LW, 2026-10-18); Patrick agreed 2026-10-18 | LW |
| [icon-cache-loader-routine] | The icon loader 0x1403FBE3C is bool Load(manager rcx, uri rdx, flag r8b), and every observed screen open passes flag 0. Its one inner lookup (0x1403FBE81) skips creation on a hit. On a miss CreateEntry 0x1403FB960 interns its own copy of the name and StartLoad 0x1403F3DAC starts the async read. Interning is keyed on content, so a later Load of the same name hits. | PROVEN | Ghidra read: the loader read from the exe, LW ledger icon-cache-heal-on-miss (LW, 2026-10-18); live watch: after a heal the screen's own Load created no second entry, one entry per cache for Defender across four caches, LW ledger icon-cache-heal-on-miss (LW, 2026-10-18); Patrick agreed 2026-10-18 | LW |
| [icon-screens-look-up-every-frame] | A screen draws each icon by looking it up every frame, about 7080 lookups a second with the inventory open, and only a screen open calls the loader, so an icon hidden under an open screen stays blank until the screen opens again. An entry still loading is skipped for that frame (0x1403E95E0). | PROVEN | live watch: the lookup trace 04:39 to 04:56, a hidden icon asked for 1360 times in 23 seconds and one load on reopen, LW ledger icon-cache-hide-reload (LW, 2026-10-18); live watch: the cost line, 7080 lookups a second in the inventory, LW ledger icon-cache-heal-on-miss (LW, 2026-10-18); live watch: the Equipment and Abilities and battle status cards went blank once their copy was hidden, owner screenshots 02:57 to 03:00, LW ledger icon-cache-scoped-hide (LW, 2026-10-18); Patrick agreed 2026-10-18 | LW |
| [icon-loads-use-stack-keys] | The game loads icons only while building a screen, one at a time per cache, and its build path looks icons up with a key built on the stack at rsp+0x20, while every draw passes a key held on the heap in a widget field. One thread can both build and draw, so the key kind, not the thread, tells a load from a draw. | PROVEN | Ghidra read: of twelve lookup call sites six are draw consumers and five build the key on the stack, 0x1403DAC6B, 0x1403DB55F, 0x1403DB658, 0x1403F49B4 and Load's inner lookup, LW ledger icon-cache-heal-on-miss (LW, 2026-10-18); live watch: the trace's longest load burst, 1770 ms for 22 icons on the inventory opening, LW ledger icon-cache-heal-on-miss (LW, 2026-10-18); live watch: guard run 07:35 to 08:36 on the dev build of 6b6d320f, thread 34 both built and drew, every load used a stack key and every draw a heap key, LW ledger icon-cache-heal-on-miss (LW, 2026-10-18); Patrick agreed 2026-10-18 | LW |
| [icon-remove-by-name-frees-entry] | The icon cache's remove by name path 0x1403DABF0 frees the entry its lookup finds. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: the remove path and its stack key lookup at 0x1403DAC6B, LW ledger icon-cache-heal-on-miss (LW, 2026-10-18) | LW |
| [icon-create-grows-slot-array-only] | CreateEntry grows only the manager's slot array at +0xE0 to +0xE8 (through 0x140031D7C), and the twelfth lookup site 0x1403FD000 is a per manager maintenance walker over a separate object vector at +0xA0 to +0xA8, so a grow during a lookup invalidates nothing a caller holds. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: CreateEntry and the twelfth lookup site read from the exe, LW ledger icon-cache-heal-on-miss (LW, 2026-10-18) | LW |
| [icon-loader-safe-from-draw-thread] | The icon loader can be called from inside a lookup miss on the draw thread, a thread the game never loads on itself: the icon loads within milliseconds and the screen's later open creates no second entry. | PROVEN | live watch: every heal fired on draw thread 39, sixteen clean across two launches, dev build of e8c5fbe6, LW ledger icon-cache-heal-on-miss (LW, 2026-10-18); live watch: guard run, fifty fast opens with hides pending and a flee with a kill, no fault, 23 heals accepted, LW ledger icon-cache-heal-on-miss (LW, 2026-10-18); Patrick agreed 2026-10-18 | LW |
| [game-reclaims-hidden-icon-entries] | The game frees parts entries whose names were hidden and reuses their slots for fresh, correctly named entries, while hidden texture records stay resident, all with no fault, so a hide leaks nothing. | PROVEN | live watch: three hidden parts slots freed and reused after several inventory opens, about two hours of menus with no fault, LW ledger icon-cache-hide-reload (LW, 2026-09-01); live watch: with the hide shipped, a battle, a save load and a relaunch passed with no fault or icon corruption, LW ledger icon-cache-hide-reload (LW, 2026-09-01); Patrick agreed 2026-09-01 | LW |
| [equip-icon-caches-outlive-battles] | Equip icon cache entries stay once drawn: 13 UITextureParts managers held them and none dropped one through menus or two battles. The inventory and weapon picker create 23 entries on open, each loaded in 100 to 200 ms, and the battle status page brings no manager of its own. | UNCERTAIN | live tape: lw427_cache_watch_20261018_022334.jsonl and lw427_cache_watch_20261018_023122.jsonl from tools/probes/lw427_icon_cache_watch.py, owner flip pending, LW ledger icon-cache-scoped-hide (LW, 2026-10-18) | LW |
| [icon-first-draw-reads-modded-pac] | The game reads an equip icon from modded.pac when a session first draws it, so a same length write into the pac shows at that first draw on both the list row and the detail card, with no restart. | PROVEN | live watch: Tideward drew Emberward's red art on both surfaces, tools/probes/live_icon_patch_probe.py, LW ledger live-icon-repaint (LW, 2026-08-16); live watch: the cold stage reproduced at the session's first equipment draw, screenshot 03:51, LW ledger live-icon-repaint (LW, 2026-08-26); live watch: Galewall changed three times in one process, LW ledger live-icon-repaint (LW, 2026-09-01); Patrick agreed 2026-09-01 | LW |
| [drawn-icon-refresh-is-conditional] | An equip icon already drawn re-reads modded.pac on the next list redraw (re-entering the list or switching party menu sections, never moving the cursor) only some of the time: it did in sessions launched fresh for the test, but hours into a session no redraw, menu, save load or title screen refreshed it, and a battle never did. | [human-bit-cleared-at-open-menu] | Clearing a unit's human control bit while its own command menu is open leaves the menu showing only End Turn. Choosing it hands the turn to the game's AI, which plays the turn whole from its start. | PROVEN | live watch: no log, by eye with the owner at the unit's attack menu (BB, 2026-10-29); live tape: live_check_act_first_walks_and_mid_turn_loss_retries_clean#2585 (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [mid-turn-auto-waits-for-menu] | Switching a unit's auto-battle byte on during its own turn shows the Auto tag at once, but its open command menu stays up until the player presses Escape, and then auto-battle takes the turn. | PROVEN | live watch: no log, the owner and a script, 0x0C written at Ramza's +0x1EC on his turn (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [item-tile-move-confirm-box] | When the player picks a move tile that holds a map item, the game first asks a Yes/No, about to move onto an item. | PROVEN | live watch: no log, the owner by eye (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [obtained-item-banner-waits-for-key] | After a unit the player moves steps onto a map item, the game's Obtained X! banner waits for F or Enter, and the game's own Maintain Auto-proceed option, checked on, does not close it. | PROVEN | live watch: no log, the owner by eye (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [item-banner-waits-for-player-side] | The item banner should wait for any unit on the player's side, guests and auto-battle units too: the pickup grants the item (0x140271CA8, 0x140266890), then opens the banner (0x14020469B, a1 0xF, wait 1) whenever byte +0x154 of the structure whose +0x148 points at the unit's record has no team bits (test at 0x140204671), with no human bit test. An auto-battle player turn did stall 120 s after a move until the owner closed an item pop-up. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: after move dispatcher 0x14020435C, bit 4 of the tile event flags (BB, 2026-10-29); live tape: auto_battle_player_side_stamped_at_setup_opus_plays_the_enemy, the pause after the player's turn at t 477 s (BB, 2026-10-29) | BB |
| [crystal-move-confirm-skips-on-auto] | The Move to this tile and obtain the crystal? Yes/No is the opener call at 0x140203D92 with a1 2 and a2 4. Its wait read 8 and the box waited when the player moved the unit by hand, and 0 with no box for the same unit on auto-battle with its Flags2 human bit still set, and for an enemy. So auto-battle skips this box, and the Flags2 human bit is not what decides it. | PROVEN | live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-10-29.jsonl, windows launch2_crystal_by_hand, launch3_resume_auto_crystal and launch1_battle (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [move-confirm-wait-from-0x154] | The move confirm, 0x140203CC8 to 0x140203D9D, takes its wait from byte +0x154 masked with 8 in the structure whose +0x148 points at the unit's battle unit record, and its a2 is a tile variant, 4 when the tile record's +0x160 has bit 1. Since an auto-battle unit got a wait of 0, auto-battle seems to clear that +0x154 bit while +0x1EE of the battle unit record keeps its human bit. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: move confirm 0x140203CC8 to 0x140203D9D (BB, 2026-10-29); live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-10-29.jsonl, wait 0 on auto with +0x1EE reading 0x08 (BB, 2026-10-29) | BB |
| [crystal-restore-box-stops-auto-battle] | The Use the crystal to fully restore HP and MP? Yes/No is the opener call at 0x1402044E3 with a1 12, a2 18 and a wait of 1, by hand and on auto-battle alike. On auto it stayed up 76 s, the UI state at 21, until the owner pressed Yes, and the turn went on with an attack. An enemy's crystal took the call at 0x140204507 (a1 12, a2 2, wait 0) and asked nothing. So any auto-battle unit on the player's side stops at this box. | PROVEN | live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-10-29.jsonl, windows launch2_crystal_by_hand, launch3_resume_auto_crystal, launch3_after_yes and launch1_battle (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [crystal-restore-question-gate] | On a crystal whose ability value (esi) is -1, the after move dispatcher asks the restore question (0x1402044E3, wait 1) only when the acting unit's +0x1EE has no team bits (0x30) and has the human bit (0x08), tested at 0x1402044AB to 0x1402044C6. Otherwise the call at 0x140204507 passes a wait of 0 and the restore applies at once through 0x1402722D8 with 2. Both calls pass the unit's slot (+0x1BC) as a2 and a3. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: after move dispatcher 0x14020435C to 0x140204A1A (BB, 2026-10-29) | BB |
| [crystal-ability-box-waits-player-side] | On a crystal holding an ability (esi not -1), the opener call at 0x14020456C waits whenever +0x1EE has no team bits, whatever the human bit says, and only an enemy gets the automatic pick (0x1402722D8 with 4). So a guest or an auto-battle unit on the player's side would get a box that waits there too. Not seen live. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: after move dispatcher 0x14020435C to 0x140204A1A (BB, 2026-10-29) | BB |
| [battle-ui-state-word] | A 32 bit UI state at 0x140C6B1CC reads 4 in normal play and went 12, 13, 16, 17, 18, 21 through a crystal move, back to 4 after the last box. 0x140203CE3 writes the 16 and 0x1402043C8 the 21. It held at 16 while the move onto the crystal Yes/No waited and at 21 while the restore HP and MP Yes/No waited. | PROVEN | live watch: six read-only snapshots of the exe's .data, run1 to run3, down-after, up-moveconfirm and up-restoreconfirm (BB, 2026-10-29); live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-10-29.jsonl, windows launch2_crystal_by_hand and launch3_resume_auto_crystal (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [ui-state-steps-on-every-move] | Every unit's move, enemies and plain tiles included, writes the UI state 16 and then 21, right after the move confirm call 0x140203D92 and the after move opener call 0x1402043C3, which passes a1 12 and a wait of 0 every time. So 16 and 21 are steps of every move, a1 12 alone does not mean a crystal, and only a 21 that holds marks a box that waits. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-10-29.jsonl, window launch2_moves (BB, 2026-10-29) | BB |
| [battle-opener-runs-every-turn] | The battle box opener 0x1402387C4 (43 call sites) serves far more than pop-ups: every unit's turn ran the same short run of calls through it, from 0x140203B60, 0x140203D92, 0x140203FB0, 0x1402043C3, 0x140204011 and 0x140202715, and the one at 0x140202715 passed a wait of 1 on every turn while play went on with no key press. So a nonzero wait alone does not mean a box that waits for a key. | PROVEN | live watch: docs/research/live_traps_2026-10-29.jsonl, every window (BB, 2026-10-29); Ghidra read: the opener's 43 call sites (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [opener-zero-wait-path] | The opener 0x1402387C4 takes the wait as its fifth argument. A wait of 0 goes straight to 0x140238360 with window id 8 and returns 1 (0x140238A96, 0x140238A87), a message that does not wait, which is what enemy units get. Any other wait picks a window id from a1, stores a1 at 0x1407DC6E8 and a2 at 0x1407DC6EC (0x140239118) and hands that id to 0x140238360. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: opener 0x1402387C4 to 0x140239141 (BB, 2026-10-29) | BB |
| [opener-window-task-4] | The routine 0x140238360 registers task 4 with the window task 0x1402381C4 through 0x1402F7DE4, which only fills the task slot and runs nothing, then writes the window id into task 4's data at 0x140D42AA0. Task data runs from 0x140D40AA0, 0x800 bytes a task, its base held in the pointer at 0x140783088. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: opener 0x1402387C4 to 0x140239141 and 0x140238360 (BB, 2026-10-29) | BB |
| [window-id-8-auto-declines] | The window task 0x1402381C4 reads its window id when the scheduler first runs it, after the opener has returned. For id 8 it draws no window and starts window record 8's handler 0x14023667C (records of 32 bytes from 0x140809960, the handler at +0x10), which marks its task data 0x42, waits two frames and writes 7 to the selection byte 0x140783290. Any other id draws its window and waits for the player's pick. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: window task 0x1402381C4 to 0x14023835D and handler 0x14023667C (BB, 2026-10-29) | BB |
| [crystal-answer-handler] | In UI state 21 the battle UI routine 0x140210AF0 calls the answer handler 0x14020BFD4 every frame (from 0x140211FCB), which through 0x140236468 and the selection resolver 0x140237FA4 copies the selection byte 0x140783290 into the answer at 0x142FD35A0. An answer of 2 applies the crystal restore through 0x1402722D8, 0, 1 and 4 hand that number to 0x1402722D8, and 7, 8 and 0xFF end the event with no reward. The asked restore comes only from an answer of 2. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: battle UI routine 0x140210AF0 and answer handler 0x14020BFD4 (BB, 2026-10-29) | BB |
| [window-id-8-write-declines-box] | Writing 8 over task 4's window id right after the opener returns, for a box it opened with a nonzero wait, should send the box down the enemy path: no window, a selection of 7 two frames later, and state 21 ending as a decline. An item banner loses nothing, the item being granted first, but a crystal then gives no reward. Not tried live. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: the opener, window task, handler and answer handler read together (BB, 2026-10-29) | BB |
