| [menu-stack-blocks-state-switch] | Each pass of the main loop calls 0x1400B458C with the menu stack object [0x143CD9E60], and bit 0 at its +0x18 skips the state switch at 0x140211B23, so an open menu blocks a state change. On Game Over it held depth 1 at +0x17 and entry 6 at +0x15. 0x1400B467C closes the top entry on a timer. A state forced past an open menu leaves a stale entry, which fits the Esc crash at 0x1402B8485. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: 0x1400B458C, the state switch at 0x140211B23 and 0x1400B467C (BB, 2026-11-04); live watch: the Game Over runs, no log named (BB, 2026-11-04) | BB |
| [boot-screen-ui-addons] | Both boot screens are UI classes outside the PSX code. UIAddonFFTOLauncher's button calls 0x1401B9054 on its agent with (agent, addon, 0, id, 0), id 0x6E being Enhanced's Start Game. UIAddonFFTOTitle's button calls 0x1401C32D0 the same way with the item id, 0 to 5 in screen order, Continue being 2. Each addon's vtable slot 12, 0x1401BA2BC and 0x1401C410C, runs about 60 to 75 times a second while its screen is up. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: the UIAddonFFTOLauncher and UIAddonFFTOTitle vtables (BB, 2026-11-04); live watch: one live read of each screen, no log named (BB, 2026-11-04) | BB |

| [movie-routine-end-bits] | The movie routine 0x1402F2E40 ends a video one of two ways: with bit 3 of +0xE0 on [0x143CD9DC8] set it waits for a key, and with it clear it ends on bit 0 of +0xE4. Setting +0xE4 bit 0 and clearing +0xE0 bit 3 ended a video, skipping the key path's call 0x1400D8DDC with no harm seen. Another video can follow on the same flags 0.04 s later, and how many a boot plays is not settled. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: the movie routine 0x1402F2E40 (BB, 2026-11-04); live watch: one outside write on the owner's screen, and a trap's two exits 0.6 s apart on a real Enter, no log named (BB, 2026-11-04); live watch: the owner's screen and the mod log, dll DB32D2D1, a skip word ending a video 2.5 s into a boot (BB, 2026-11-04) | BB |

The unit menu is the first list of the command menu object at [[[[0x143CD9DA8]+0x10]+0x40]+0x48], with its item count at +0x358, the mouse's item at +0x460, the click pending byte at +0x469 and its menu id, 0, at +0x470. Writing item 4 and pending 1 opened Auto-battle, and item 2 picked Wait, each within 50 ms, with the pad word at [[0x1408B9A48]] never written. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: reads and frames, two sessions on Mandalia Plain, sheets last-20261105-1530 to 1551 in F:\FFTRec\sheets (BB, 2026-11-05); source read: FFTHandsFree moving the unit menu's cursor through +0x460, 2026-06-23 (BB, 2026-11-05) | BB |

The tactic list behind Auto-battle, at [[[[0x143CD9DA8]+0x10]+0x48]+0x48], takes a click the same way with its item at +0x150 and pending at +0x159, and item 1 there chose Attack Enemy. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: 0x1402AD794 and 0x1400E64B4 (BB, 2026-11-05); live watch: reads and frames, the tactic list's item 1 choosing Attack Enemy, F:\FFTRec\sheets (BB, 2026-11-05) | BB |

| [flavor-line-overwrite-displays] | An equip card's description text draws from heap buffers that can be overwritten in place: a same length string written over a weapon's flavor line showed on the card, wrapped by the renderer, and held through reopening, scrolling and a battle, other cards untouched. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: Patrick eyewitness and screenshot 08:15, Cutpurse card, weapon id 1, 3 sites written, log flavor-spike 3 written 0 skipped, on a build before 1.5.2, no owner flip named, SELF_TEST_PLAN Appendix A, LW ledger flavor-line-overwrite-displays (LW, 2026-07-05) | LW |
| [attack-row-catalog-offset-redirect] | The battle Attack row's name and its hover card title come from one name offset in the command text catalog: pointing it at other text renames both at any length, the row shrinking to fit and the title scrolling, and the description follows its own offset beside it. The menu reads it as it builds, so a change shows on the next open. | PROVEN | live watch: Evasive Stance and then a 58 character line drew in the row and title, Monosnap 01.51.32, poke and restore fine on all 3 copies, probes/attack_row_redirect.py, owner eyewitness, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger attack-row-catalog-offset-redirect (LW, 2026-07-06); live watch: the shipped rename drew the weapon name on the first turn of the session's first battle and followed a mid battle weapon swap, smoke row 5.3, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger attack-row-catalog-offset-redirect (LW, 2026-07-14); Patrick agreed 2026-07-14 | LW |
| [command-text-catalog-layout] | The command text catalog is a string pool under a table of 0x24 byte records, nine u32s each (nameOff, descOff, poolHeadOff, id, poolHead8Off, three zeros, ordinal), offsets counted from the record. Attack is record id 1 at its label minus 0x1FC1, with nameOff 0x1FC1 and descOff 0x1FC8, the only field that references the label. | UNCERTAIN | game table: probes/attack_table_scan.py, the same constants on 3 of 3 copies across two launches and a whole address sweep finding one reference to the label, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger attack-row-catalog-offset-redirect (LW, 2026-07-06) | LW |

| [uistatuseffect-key-is-bit-plus-one] | Status names and captions live in nxd/uistatuseffect.en.nxd inside the encrypted data/enhanced/0004.en.pac, 45 rows, Key being the status bit index plus 1 (Berserk bit 20 is Key 21). Keys 1, 15 and 32, bit 0, the bit 14 called Cursed and Wall's bit 31, ship with no name or caption. | UNCERTAIN | game table: unpacked with audit_nxd_bakes.unpack and lib/nxd.decode_nxd_to_sqlite, the Key rule checked on five statuses, LW ledger invented-status-id0-mark (LW, 2026-07-22) | LW |

### Graphics and palettes

| Id | Fact | Mark | Evidence | Leaned on by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [swing-prep-copies-swing-id-word] | When a unit swings, the swing prep routine 0x140281CE8 copies the standing swing id word at 0x1407B077A into the swing's animation record (movzx at 0x140282073, then mov [rdi+0x20],ax), and the art routine 0x14026A358 draws the blade of whatever id arrives. On a swing at an empty tile with an extended id that word is 0, so the unit swings bare handed. | PROVEN | live watch: a stub hand planted at the site drew Ravager's own blade at an empty tile, and an earlier cut using another unit's hand drew the Akademy Blade, tools/probes/lw365_swingid_fallback_probe.py, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger swing-id-fallback-stub (LW, 2026-08-31); live watch: the call logger lw365_clone_call_log.py saw the art routine receive 0 for the Ravager and 257 for the Akademy Blade, LW ledger swing-id-fallback-stub (LW, 2026-08-31); Ghidra read: site bytes 0F B7 05 00 E7 52 00, LW ledger swing-id-fallback-stub (LW, 2026-08-31); Patrick agreed 2026-08-31 | LW |
| [range-index-minus-one-punches] | When the range index accessor 0x1402B8BCC answers -1 for an item id, as it does for ids it has no row for, the attack animation setup 0x1403099B0 publishes no weapon and the unit punches. With that accessor and the sprite pair accessor 0x1402B8E60 answering for a real weapon, the unit swings that weapon's blade. | PROVEN | live watch: both cloned to the Chaos Blade, id 261 drew a knight sword blade on every attack, screenshots tools/probes/lw346_moonblade_swing_blade_1.png and _2.png, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger capbreak-swing-art-via-accessor-clones (LW, 2026-08-27); live watch: re-observed on the ported build 18:37 to 18:42, LW ledger capbreak-swing-art-via-accessor-clones (LW, 2026-08-27); Ghidra read: static read of the live process, journal 2026-08-27 00:00 to 00:50, LW ledger capbreak-swing-art-via-accessor-clones (LW, 2026-08-27); Patrick agreed 2026-08-27 | LW |
| [attack-weapon-word-write-chain] | The weapon word in a unit's action block (+0x1A8 of its battle unit record, published to 0x1407B077A) comes by memcpy from a stack local the action commit routine 0x140281488 builds. Its class 8 path copies its input unchanged from the plain attack builder 0x14030D2D4, which reads the static template 0x14186AFAC. During id 261's swings the word read 242 on both bare handed and blade swings. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: a Cheat Engine find what writes on the word resolved to the CRT memcpy, LW ledger capbreak-swing-art-via-accessor-clones (LW, 2026-08-27); live watch: a 0.5 ms watch, tools/probes/lw346_render_cluster_watch.py, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger capbreak-swing-art-via-accessor-clones (LW, 2026-08-27); Ghidra read: the commit routine and attack builder read from the live process, the template filled by copy protected code at 0x1508B2DC0 on the Denuvo build, LW ledger capbreak-swing-art-via-accessor-clones (LW, 2026-08-27) | LW |
| [wep-spr-palette-block] | Battle weapon colour comes from the 512 byte palette block at the head of FFTPack file 71, unit/battle_wep_spr.bin (16 palettes of 16 BGR555, colour 0 transparent). The HD layer upscales the classic art and indexes these palettes, so a modded copy of the file changes true hues rather than shuffling indices. | PROVEN | live watch: round 12, the loader log showed 2 modded reads of file 71 and none of the game's copy, and Ramza's swung sword drew flat magenta at hue 300.0 in one blade band of 738 pixels, tools/probes/lw251_wep_spr_forge.py, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger wep-spr-palette-block (LW, 2026-08-19); Patrick agreed 2026-08-19 | LW |
| [weapon-palette-from-battle-bin-record] | Which palette a weapon draws from is the high nibble of byte 0 of its BATTLE.BIN item graphics record at 0x02D3E6 + (itemId - 1) * 2. The low nibble is the swing arc palette and byte 1 the graphic, read against the item's category. battle_bin is fftpack/battle_bin.en.bin in data/classic/0002.en.pac, FFTPack index 0, 1397096 bytes. | PROVEN | live watch: with all sixteen palettes forged to label hues four swords matched the file, Broadsword 14, Iron Sword 3, Mythril Sword 15 and Sleep Blade 15, LW ledger wep-spr-palette-block (LW, 2026-08-19); game table: the record read by tools/probes/lw289_battle_bin_palette_map.py, map in tools/probes/lw289_weapon_palette_map.json, LW ledger wep-spr-palette-block (LW, 2026-08-19); PSX doc: the FFHacktics BATTLE.BIN item graphics record, fifteen published records matching the shipped file byte for byte, LW ledger wep-spr-palette-block (LW, 2026-08-19); Patrick agreed 2026-08-19 | LW |
| [weapon-and-effect-palettes-disjoint] | Weapons draw only from palettes 3 to 15 and swing arcs and effects only from 0 to 2, with no overlap across all 127 weapons, so repainting a weapon palette never retints an arc. Palettes 1 and 2 hold ink only at slots 11 to 15. The 127 weapons share 13 palettes, 5 to 20 each. | UNCERTAIN | game table: BATTLE.BIN with X in use 3 to 15 and Y in use 0 to 2, Y 0 on 107 weapons and 1 and 2 on ten each, LW ledger wep-spr-palette-block (LW, 2026-08-19); game table: a connected component pass over battle_wep_spr.bin, LW ledger wep-spr-palette-block (LW, 2026-08-19) | LW |
| [wep-spr-file-three-pages] | battle_wep_spr.bin is 85504 bytes in three pages, each after its own 512 byte palette bank: palA at 0x0000 then page 1 at 0x0200 (rows 0 to 255, weapons), palB at 0x8200 (identical to palA) then page 2 at 0x8400 (rows 256 to 511, arcs and sparkles), palC at 0x10400 (near black additive fades) then page 3 at 0x10600 (rows 512 to 655, impacts). | UNCERTAIN | game table: battle_wep_spr.bin from 0002.pac, LW ledger wep-spr-palette-block (LW, 2026-08-19); game table: the loaded file's three format landmarks read in memory, palB matching palA and palC a different bank, LW ledger resident-weapon-palette-buffer (LW, 2026-08-21) | LW |
| [weapon-palette-zones-overlap] | No two weapon graphics sharing a palette use disjoint colour indices: of 681 such pairs the least overlap is 3 indices and all collide in the base zone 1 to 4, so in vanilla one palette means one colour scheme. Each full palette is four short ramps plus a shared specular at slot 15. | UNCERTAIN | game table: palette bytes and pixel data of battle_wep_spr.bin, LW ledger wep-spr-palette-block (LW, 2026-08-19) | LW |
| [battle-rereads-wep-spr-each-load] | The game reads battle_wep_spr.bin (file 71) again at battle loads and keeps no copy for the whole session: the file swapped on disk between two battles of one session showed in the second, and the loader logged a modded read of file 71 at each of three battle loads. Which loads read the file again and which reuse the copy already loaded is open, see [battle-load-copies-weapon-palettes]. | PROVEN | live watch: round 14, the file swapped on disk between two battles of one session and battle 2 drew normal steel, three MODDED reads of file 71 at 07:59:35, 08:02:04 and 08:02:41, tools/probes/lw289_palette_selector.py, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger wep-spr-palette-block (LW, 2026-08-21); Patrick agreed 2026-08-21 | LW |
| [battle-load-copies-weapon-palettes] | At a battle load the game copies the weapon palettes from the loaded battle_wep_spr.bin, a heap copy whose address changes each launch and that nothing points to, into the static 1024 byte workspace at 0x140d35750 (two 512 byte banks, the second at 0x140d35950). A workspace write lasts only until the next battle load, and a write to the heap copy showed after a battle reloaded from the world map, so that reload used the heap copy, not the file on disk. | PROVEN | live watch: palette 14 written magenta in the loaded file, the Cutpurse drew magenta after a battle reload with Galewind unchanged and all four addresses then read magenta, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger resident-weapon-palette-buffer (LW, 2026-08-21); live watch: cyan written to the workspace's bank A only, both palette 14 knives drew normal steel after a reload and both banks read vanilla, LW ledger resident-weapon-palette-buffer (LW, 2026-08-21); Ghidra read: a memset(0x140d35750, 0, 0x400) clears the whole workspace, LW ledger resident-weapon-palette-buffer (LW, 2026-08-21); proven in LW 2026-08-24 | LW |
| [weapon-palette-sampled-per-draw] | The game reads the weapon palette workspace at 0x140d35750 on every draw: a write mid battle recolours the next swing at once with no reload, and two palettes written two colours show both in the same battle. Whether a swing latches its palette at animation start is untested. | PROVEN | live watch: round 1, palette 14 set cyan in both banks, the Cutpurse swung cyan while the Galewind on palette 4 swung steel, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger per-weapon-colour-by-turn-repaint (LW, 2026-08-21); live watch: round 2, palettes 14 and 4 set yellow and magenta together and both showed, owner screenshot, LW ledger per-weapon-colour-by-turn-repaint (LW, 2026-08-21); proven in LW 2026-08-24 | LW |
| [parry-draws-two-weapons-at-once] | A battle weapon is drawn only during its wielder's attack animation, with idle units showing none, except that a parry draws the attacker's blade and the parrying unit's raised blade in the same frame. | PROVEN | live watch: with all 13 weapon palettes painted distinct, an owner screenshot showed a ninja's blade and Ramza's parrying blade in two colours beside the Weapon parry! callout, LW ledger per-weapon-colour-by-turn-repaint (LW, 2026-08-21); live watch: idle units showed no weapon in both round screenshots, LW ledger per-weapon-colour-by-turn-repaint (LW, 2026-08-21); proven in LW 2026-08-24 | LW |
| [thrown-weapons-skip-weapon-palettes] | A thrown weapon (a ninja throwing Gloomfang) and the Move-Find treasure staff draw from a source the 13 weapon palettes do not reach, while samurai ability animations draw the weapon with its own palette. | PROVEN | live watch: with all 13 weapon palettes painted the throw and the staff drew normal steel, LW ledger per-weapon-colour-by-turn-repaint (LW, 2026-08-21); proven in LW 2026-08-24 | LW |
| [g2d-equipment-sheet-override] | The HD art for a battle weapon such as a bow is g2d entry 161, a 512x512 sheet of 4 bit palette indices (bows, guns, shields, helmets, harps, maces, blades, spears, shuriken, cloths), the classic battle_wep_spr.bin weapon page at twice the size with index 0 transparent. A loose system/ffto/g2d/tex_161.bin of raw decompressed bytes replaces it from the next launch. The crossbow is not on it. | PROVEN | live watch: a whole sheet index derangement made an Archer's bow draw deep blue with a white edge while the nocked arrow stayed vanilla, tools/probes/lw251_g2d_extract.py, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger g2d-equipment-sheet-override (LW, 2026-08-18); live watch: Reloaded logs show entry 161 served once per launch at the menu or formation screen, a reason the retraction gave to re-examine this row, LW ledger g2d-clut-bank-override (LW, 2026-08-19); Patrick agreed 2026-08-18 | LW |
| [g2d-container-format] | g2d.dat (magic YOX) holds the HD 2D art: 1426 entries by its first read, an offset table at 0xaa4800 whose offsets drift +16 per index, and each entry a YOX header plus zlib. The table's third word is 6 on every entry, and each payload carries 16 unknown trailing bytes inside payload_len. | UNCERTAIN | game table: g2d.dat read by tools/probes/lw251_g2d_extract.py, LW ledger g2d-equipment-sheet-override (LW, 2026-08-18); game table: the adversarial review's read of the entry table, which counted 2450 entries in a shipped copy, LW ledger g2d-equipment-sheet-override (LW, 2026-08-19) | LW |
| [itemdata-palette-byte-inert] | The ItemData per item palette byte has no effect on battle weapon art, even written live across battle reloads. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: round 6, the byte written live with no change in battle art, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger g2d-equipment-sheet-override (LW, 2026-08-18); live watch: shown inert separately in 2026-06, LW ledger wep-spr-palette-block (LW, 2026-08-19) | LW |
| [g2d-clut-bank-override] | g2d entry 156 is a battle weapon palette bank, and a loose tex_156.bin repaints battle weapon colours. | CONTRADICTED | live watch: zero Accessing file 156 lines across all 18 Reloaded logs on disk while the same sized entries 154 and 155 are served routinely, LW ledger g2d-clut-bank-override (LW, 2026-08-19); live watch: round 11 shipped tex_156 and the game read neither 156 nor the control 158, LW ledger g2d-clut-bank-override (LW, 2026-08-19); game table: entry 156 is a 96x96 4bpp sprite, the hatch swirl of the chocobo egg family 154 to 163, LW ledger g2d-clut-bank-override (LW, 2026-08-19) | LW |

| [anim-request-register] | A unit plays any animation page when its render node's request word ([battle-render-node-layout]) gets the page id plus one: the request latches with no hold, a frame of duration 0 freezes, and the unit's next real move re-stamps it. | PROVEN | live watch: two input pokes, one consumed before the 0.10 s sample leaving the unit frozen in the pose and one with req 53 held through 0.25 s and eaten by 0.50 s, the unit visibly playing the page both times, owner live, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger anim-request-register (LW, 2026-07-21); Patrick agreed 2026-07-21 | LW |

### Saves and loading

| Id | Fact | Mark | Evidence | Leaned on by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [order-templates-are-save-state] | The two weapon menu order charts are saved with the game and restored from the save on load, never rebuilt from what is owned: load apply copies both out of the save struct (0x14021B4BD, struct+0x8A6C) and the serializer writes them back (0x1402194BC). So a save written before an id was seated restores a chart without it, and a stale id a save carries returns every load. | PROVEN | Ghidra read: the load apply and serializer sites, LW ledger order-templates-are-save-state (LW, 2026-08-30); live watch: re-test 5 disconfirmed the earlier pre write theory, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger order-templates-are-save-state (LW, 2026-08-30); live watch: id 261 still in both display order tables after save, quit and load, tools/probes/lw346_saveload_check.py, LW ledger capbreak-save-roundtrip-1-5-2 (LW, 2026-08-27); Patrick agreed 2026-08-31 | LW |
| [save-keeps-261-bag-counts] | The save keeps exactly 261 bag counts, ids 0 to 260 at save+0x83A8, so a count past 260 is neither saved nor cleared by a load, while a roster hand holding an id past 260 and the Acquired list ride the save. The serializer is 0x14021926C and the restores 0x14021B1D5 and 0x14021E1D1. | PROVEN | live watch: a count of 2 read 2 after a same process reload and 1, the rig's boot seed, after a cold boot and load, with the hand still 261, tools/probes/lw346_saveload_check.py, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger capbreak-save-roundtrip-1-5-2 (LW, 2026-08-27); live watch: re-observed on the ported build's cold boot at 18:43, LW ledger capbreak-save-roundtrip-1-5-2 (LW, 2026-08-27); Ghidra read: the serializer and both restores read live, journal 2026-08-27 00:00 to 00:50, LW ledger capbreak-save-roundtrip-1-5-2 (LW, 2026-08-27); Patrick agreed 2026-08-27 | LW |
| [load-empties-unknown-hand-ids] | On a game without a cap break, loading a save that holds an item id past 260 in a hand empties that hand (it reads 0x00FF), rebuilds the Acquired list without the id and loads cleanly, while the two display order tables keep the id. The emptying is the load sanitizer's 5 slot validity loop behind the validity accessor 0x1402B8EBC. | PROVEN | live watch: hands 255 and 255, count 0, the tables carrying 261 at index 127 with the marker at 128 and Acquired without 261, tools/probes/lw346_saveload_check.py, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger capbreak-uninstall-is-clean-loss (LW, 2026-08-27); Patrick agreed 2026-08-27 | LW, HF |
| [resumed-battle-not-rebuilt] | A battle resumed from a suspend save restores its units without rebuilding them: in such a launch CopyJobEffectsToUnit never reached the player's slot 16, so Ramza was not built through the deploy routine. | PROVEN | live watch: brain/probe_20261029_045726.jsonl, a launch the owner confirmed resumed a suspend save, a log no longer kept (BB, 2026-10-29); live watch: brain/probe_20261029_055737.jsonl #9 to #12, by eye, a log no longer kept (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [suspend-save-keeps-auto-byte] | A suspend save keeps each unit's auto-battle byte +0x1EC: resuming one made while Ramza was on auto brought him back on auto, his first call at 24 s a computer turn with nothing written that battle. Only a save made with auto off comes back without it. | PROVEN | live watch: brain/probe_20261029_055737.jsonl #9 to #12, by eye, a log no longer kept (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [continue-restores-saved-state-block] | A Continue from the title restores the saved state block through the save and restore routine 0x1401D46B0, edx 1 to restore, called from 0x1402CE7A8 by 0x140210D04 when 0x142FF9E2C is 1. It writes the state 0x140C6B1CC, 75 in the one trapped load, and the exit code 0x140D3A848. That load ended about 13 s later through the exit routine with code 8, but another kept the exit code at 0 throughout, so the exit code is no load signal. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: one trapped Continue, docs/research/title_continue_2026-11-04.jsonl (BB, 2026-11-04); Ghidra read: 0x1401D46B0, whose middle 0x1401D479A was first taken for the routine (BB, 2026-11-04); live watch: one run's reads from launch, the exit code at 0 through the load, no log named (BB, 2026-11-04) | BB |
| [autosave-at-menu-and-world-map] | The game keeps its autosave in autoenhanced.png in the save folder, apart from the manual saves in enhanced.png, and writes it when a hand played unit's menu opens and on reaching the world map, not when a battle starts. Continue resumes the newest one, in a battle if it was written there: a game closed at a unit's menu came back in that battle, and Continue after a clean leave landed on the world map. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: save file times and one kill mid battle (BB, 2026-11-04); live watch: file times beside the reads, autoenhanced.png at 15:33:05, 15:34:21, 15:35:19, 15:49:02 and 15:51:38 (BB, 2026-11-05) | BB |
| [suspend-save-restores-spawned-copy] | A suspend save keeps a unit spawned into a battle: after a restart it restored a spawned copy. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: the owner's screen, video F:\FFTRec\kept\spawn\copy-crystal-game-over-20261104.mp4 (BB, 2026-11-04) | BB |
| [retry-from-start-keeps-board] | Retry from Start of Battle keeps the units, signs and tiles and comes back at Ramza's first turn, with slot 2 already moved in the one retry read. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: reads, encounter 30 (BB, 2026-11-05) | BB |
| [retry-from-formation-rebuilds-units] | Retry from Formation rebuilds the units from a copy: job, level, PA, MA, Speed, Brave, Faith, gear and signs come back as they were, a sign written during the battle is undone, and HP, status, experience and the clocks reset. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: memory scans of the owner's retry, snap_formation_before, _screen and _first_turn, encounter 30, five units (BB, 2026-11-05) | BB |
| [continue-resumes-same-battle] | After the game is killed mid battle, Continue lands in the same battle with its unit records as they stood: seven kills in a Mandalia battle, 6 to 15 s in, all came back there, and a unit given job 118 and sheet 0xB8 by a seed kept both. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: a record read after a kill, no seed file, pid 39872 (BB, 2026-11-06); live watch: reads over seven kills, restart_proof_2026-11-07 (BB, 2026-11-07) | BB |
| [resume-reopens-unit-menu] | Resuming a battle killed with a unit's menu open reopens that menu within 0.4 s of the boot ending. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: the mod's log at 01:29:39 and reads, restart_proof_2026-11-07 (BB, 2026-11-07) | BB |
| [save-serializer-and-load-apply] | Every save goes through the serializer 0x140218F78, which the save wrapper 0x14021B070 reaches by a tail jump, and a load applies through 0x14021B0E8. Both work on the save struct the pointer at 0x140D407A0 names, normally the static buffer 0x142C81C80, so the struct is not transient. | PROVEN | Ghidra read: the load apply reading the global as 4C 8B 05 98 56 B2 00 at 0x14021B101 and the tail jmp at 0x14021B0E3, with tools/probes/lw346_xref_scan.py finding 46 references to 0x140D407A0 and none to 0x141D407A0, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger save-edge-hooks-key-bag-counts (LW, 2026-08-27); live watch: hooks on both routines fired on the owner's saves and loads in the 15:18 to 15:24 re-test, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger save-edge-hooks-key-bag-counts (LW, 2026-08-30); Patrick agreed 2026-08-30 | LW |
| [save-header-identifies-save] | The save struct's header, +0x100 to +0x1B8, holds the play time at +0x1B4 and the slot list data, and reads the same when a save is written and when it is loaded, except +0x11A, +0x11C and +0x11D, which read 0xFF right after the serializer returns and 0 at rest. | PROVEN | live watch: tools/probes/lw353_header_roundtrip.py's resting header captures at 01:09:27 and 01:09:53 reproduced all five logged save keys with those three bytes set to 0xFF, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger save-edge-hooks-key-bag-counts (LW, 2026-08-28); live watch: three loads in the re-test each matched the key its own save wrote, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger save-edge-hooks-key-bag-counts (LW, 2026-08-30); Patrick agreed 2026-08-30 | LW |
| [load-restores-rebuild-bag] | Both restore routines rebuild the bag from the save struct after zeroing count[0]: the load apply 0x14021B0E8, with its store at 0x14021B1C8 and copy at 0x14021B1D5, and a second restore 0x14021DE98, with its copy at 0x14021E1D1, which never fired live, so what drives it is unknown. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: disasm of both routines, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger save-edge-hooks-key-bag-counts (LW, 2026-08-27); live watch: the second restore's own canary stayed silent through the owner's runs, LW ledger save-edge-hooks-key-bag-counts (LW, 2026-08-30) | LW |
| [save-async-file-stepper] | 0x14021DDF0 is the game's async file operation stepper, and it runs for saves as well as loads. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: the static read of the save edges, on the Denuvo build, LW ledger save-edge-hooks-key-bag-counts (LW, 2026-08-27) | LW |
| [hidden-unit-survives-autosave] | A unit hidden by its gate byte when the game autosaves stays hidden in the battle resumed from that save. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: observed in the hide session, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger hide-reveal-ghost-statue (LW, 2026-07-10) | LW |

### Game files and the modloader

| Id | Fact | Mark | Evidence | Leaned on by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [modded-pac-icon-storage] | Inside modded.pac each menu icon .tex sits uncompressed, verbatim and exactly once, at the loose file's own length (cards 0xC860 bytes, small icons 0x3060), so a same length overwrite in place is well defined. The dataOffset in modded_files.txt is section relative, not a file offset, and the pac stays writable while the game holds it open. | PROVEN | live watch: tools/probes/live_icon_patch_probe.py located each icon by a content search with one match and patched it live, LW ledger live-icon-repaint (LW, 2026-08-16); Patrick agreed 2026-09-01 | LW |
| [modded-pac-merge-is-incremental] | The modloader's launch merge into modded.pac is incremental: a write into the pac survives relaunches until the matching loose file changes, so a relaunch does not repair a patched pac. | PROVEN | live watch: after a mid probe relaunch Emberward's art counted twice and Galewall's bytes were gone, with modded_files.txt untouched since Aug 19 while the pac's mtime moved, LW ledger live-icon-repaint (LW, 2026-08-26); Patrick agreed 2026-09-01 | LW |
| [modloader-override-channels] | The modloader serves mod files three ways: fftpack files through a read detour that opens the mod's file fresh on every read with no cache, system/ffto/g2d/tex_N.bin from a buffer cache filled once per index per process, and the rest, nxd and ui .tex included, merged into modded.pac once at startup, which the game reads in place of loose files. A mod's whole system/ffto/g2d.dat is also served, through hooks on CFILE_DAT::Load and Decode. | UNCERTAIN | source read: decompiled fftivc.utility.modloader.dll, FFTOModPackManager.Apply and _cachedFileBuffers, LW ledger live-icon-repaint (LW, 2026-08-16); source read: FFTPackFileOverrideStrategy.OnRequestRead does File.OpenRead per call, LW ledger wep-spr-palette-block (LW, 2026-08-21); live watch: a Reloaded log showing g2d.dat added and 0xCF6120 bytes served from the mod file, never validly tested for effect, LW ledger g2d-equipment-sheet-override (LW, 2026-08-19) | LW, CC |
| [modloader-log-serve-lines] | The modloader's log tells a real serve from a registration. FFTPack logs [FFTPack] Accessing file N for the game's own copy and Accessing MODDED file N for a mod's. For g2d, mapping G2D file N is written at mod load either way, and [G2D] Accessing file N only when the CFILE_DAT hook serves the entry. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: 18 Reloaded logs on disk, entry 156 mapped every launch and never accessed, LW ledger g2d-clut-bank-override (LW, 2026-08-19); live watch: the round 12 launch log with 2 modded reads and 0 game copy reads of file 71, LW ledger wep-spr-palette-block (LW, 2026-08-19) | LW |
| [reloaded-hook-jmp-abs32-patch] | Reloaded patches a hooked function's entry with a 7 byte jmp [abs32] (FF 24 25), padded with NOPs to the next instruction boundary. | PROVEN | live watch: no log, a live read of the three installed hooks (BB, 2026-10-29); Patrick agreed 2026-10-29 | BB |
| [hd-map-files-by-number] | The game's drawing side loads its own map files by map number: bg/meshes/map_%03d_mesh.bin, bg/anims/map_%03d_anim.bin and bg/textures/%03d/%s.tga. The mesh loader and the map loader's next step both skip maps 15 and 33 by number. | UNCERTAIN | Ghidra read: the exe's strings and the mesh loader 0x1400C7F54 (BB, 2026-11-05) | BB |
| [reloaded-launch-flag-loads-mods] | Starting Reloaded-II.exe with --launch brings the game up with its mods loaded and no Cheat Engine open, and launch to the world map took about 22 s. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: reads and the mod's log, eight starts from 01:24, restart_proof_2026-11-07, the first pid 16684 (BB, 2026-11-07) | BB |
| [table-xml-whole-row-writeback] | The modloader applies every table row a mod's XML lists as a whole row written back over its startup snapshot, so a one field edit reverts any runtime write another mod made to that row, JobCommandId included since loader 1.7.1. Load order cannot fix it, only listing fewer rows can. | PROVEN | source read: Nenkai fftivc.utility.modloader FFTOJobDataManager.ApplyTablePatch, each field model.X ?? previous.X against the OnAllModsLoaded snapshot, LW ledger table-xml-whole-row-writeback (LW, 2026-07-14); live watch: deleting LW's row 57 block from JobData.xml brought Red Mage's abilities back on relaunch, Blue And Red Mages 2.0.2, owner ladder, LW ledger table-xml-whole-row-writeback (LW, 2026-07-14); proven in LW 2026-07-14 | LW |

### The exe and its builds

| Id | Fact | Mark | Evidence | Leaned on by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| [denuvo-era-code-forms-differ] | Some code in the Denuvo era builds uses other instruction forms than the plain MSVC compile of 1.5.2 no-DRM (xor esi,esi as 31 F6 not 33 F6, mov rbx,rdx as 48 89 D3 not 48 8B DA), so a byte pattern or code address taken from those builds can miss on no-DRM, as fftivc.together's 1.5.0 BattleRoutine pattern and FFTHandsFree's DoAction entry do. The RVAs Living Weapons pinned on the 1.5.2 Denuvo build all carried over, see [denuvo-free-build-relocates-under-aslr]. | UNCERTAIN | exe scan: fftivc.together's 1.5.0 BattleRoutine pattern, zero hits on 1.5.2 no-DRM and one hit in MSVC forms (BB, 2026-10-26); exe scan: FFTHandsFree's DoAction entry 0x1402832F8 is no prologue on no-DRM (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB |
| [doaction-entry-0x1402832f8] | DoAction, FFTHandsFree's action entry, starts at preferred 0x1402832F8 on 1.5.2 no-DRM, where FFTHandsFree verified it live on 1.5. | CONTRADICTED | exe scan: the bytes there are 2f 3d 85 01 on 1.5.2 no-DRM, not a prologue (BB, 2026-10-26); source read: FFTHandsFree, live verified on 1.5 (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB, HF |
| [doaction-body-lea-two-hits] | The lea rdi,[rcx+1A0h] that opens DoAction's body appears twice in the 1.5.2 no-DRM exe, at RVA 0x28329B and RVA 0x30D100. | UNCERTAIN | exe scan: the lea's byte pattern (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB, HF |
| [symbol-map-addresses-other-build] | The code addresses in skeewirt/fftivc-symbol-map belong to another build: on 1.5.2 no-DRM its function entries land mid instruction under every transform tried. Its names still serve. | UNCERTAIN | exe scan: thunks 0x30A468 and 0x2F8548 and the entries of Battle_UnitCommandMode 0x1F76B8, Battle_ActionCommandSelectMode 0x1F20A4, BattleRoutine_ProcessMoveFlagAndTileInfo 0x30AC64 and MarkBattleUnitMovedAndCheckIfCanAct 0x2F2438, under four transforms (BB, 2026-10-26) | BB |
| [denuvo-free-build-relocates-under-aslr] | The Denuvo free 1.5.2 build (Steam buildid 24304444, image stamp 0x6A5E4A1B, size 0x03F9E000) loads at a random base every launch, and every RVA pinned on the Denuvo build is unchanged, so each preferred address works once rebased onto the live image base. | PROVEN | live watch: the hotfix 2.3.4 pass at base 0x7FF65E0F0000 armed with a Chaos Blade kill credited, LW ledger denuvo-free-build-relocates-under-aslr (LW, 2026-10-16); live watch: the main pass at base 0x7FF65FC60000, extended inventory armed with both relocation pages, Warbrand listed, equipped and swung, LW ledger denuvo-free-build-relocates-under-aslr (LW, 2026-10-16); exe scan: an offline exe diff, docs/research/PORT_1.5.2_NODRM_OFFSETS.md, LW ledger denuvo-free-build-relocates-under-aslr (LW, 2026-10-16); Patrick agreed 2026-10-16 | LW, BB, TM |
| [globals-past-raw-data-read-zero] | Several globals sit in .data past its raw file data (raw 0x1A3A00 of virtual 0x35A87FC), so they read 0 from the moment the exe maps until the game writes them: the pause flag 0x140C6B1C8, the submenu flag 0x140D4080C, the roster at 0x1411A7D10, the world map menu byte 0x140D508D0 and the party browse byte 0x140D408E2. | PROVEN | exe scan: tools/probes/sentinel_file_image_probe.py against stamp 0x6A5E4A1B, LW ledger sentinels-file-backed-idle-at-map-time (LW, 2026-10-17); exe scan: tools/probes/twin_flags_file_image_probe.py against stamp 0x6A5E4A1B, LW ledger twin-menu-flags-zero-at-map-time (LW, 2026-10-17); live watch: two cold launches at 00:40:38 and 00:50:44 armed the sensing lane on its first step with no boot grace line, LW ledger sentinels-file-backed-idle-at-map-time (LW, 2026-10-17); Patrick agreed 2026-10-17 | LW, TM, HF |
| [sentinels-file-backed-idle-values] | In the exe file the slot0 word 0x140782A30 and the slot9 word 0x140782A54 hold 0xFFFFFFFF, the event id 0x140782A94 holds 0xFFFF and the battle mode byte 0x1409069A0 holds 0xFF, so they read those idle values from boot, and two cold launches showed nothing else before the title screen. | PROVEN | exe scan: tools/probes/sentinel_file_image_probe.py against stamp 0x6A5E4A1B, all four file backed, LW ledger sentinels-file-backed-idle-at-map-time (LW, 2026-10-17); live watch: two cold launches armed the lane on its first step with no boot grace line and no sensing warning, LW ledger sentinels-file-backed-idle-at-map-time (LW, 2026-10-17); Patrick agreed 2026-10-17 | LW, BB, TM, HF, MP |
| [jobcommand-table-file-baked] | The job command table is baked into the exe file, so it is in memory from the moment the module maps, before any save loads. On 1.5.1 the record 8 and 9 pair sat at file offset 0x67D6DB and was unique within 4 MB either side of its address. | UNCERTAIN | exe scan: tools/probes/anchorscan_feasibility_probe.py exe mode on the 1.5.1 backup, LW ledger anchorscan-tier1-jobcommand-pair (LW, 2026-07-14); live watch: exactly one pair hit at the pin, and in the owner's drill the shipped scout found it at its pin from the title screen before any save, lw82-drill-livingweapon.log, on 1.5.1, LW ledger anchorscan-tier1-jobcommand-pair (LW, 2026-07-14) | LW |
| [patch-1-5-static-shift] | The 1.5 recompile moved static data by up to +0x6C3C, the largest delta LW observed: the party roster went from 0x1411A18D0 before 1.5 to 0x1411A7D10 on 1.5 and 1.5.1. | UNCERTAIN | source read: LW's note of the 1.5 recompile's largest observed static delta, how it was measured not stated, LW ledger anchorscan-tier1-jobcommand-pair (LW, 2026-07-14); source read: the two known roster bases, LW ledger anchorscan-tier2-roster-structural-confirm (LW, 2026-07-14) | LW |
| [ability-tables-writable-pages] | The ability action table and the inflict status table sit on writable pages: a plain WriteProcessMemory lands on both table bases, ability 189's +15 byte and inflict row 29, with no VirtualProtect needed. | UNCERTAIN | live watch: tools/probes/page_protect_probe.py, owner run, on a build before 1.5.2, LW ledger cut-ability-provoke-recipe (LW, 2026-07-22) | LW |
